```
1	"""
2	GPTQ + NVFP4 + FourOverSix quantization for MiniCPM-SALA submission.
3	
4	GPTQ (Hessian-based optimal weight rounding) with FourOverSix adaptive block
5	scale selection. Calibration: 128 wikitext samples, log-uniform length distribution
6	(512-64K tokens, 8 buckets x 16 samples). Max seq length 48K.
7	
8	Local verified accuracy: 79.98% with dense-as-sparse.
9	
10	Usage (called by prepare_model.sh):
11	    python preprocess_model.py --input <src> --output <dst>
12	"""
13	from __future__ import annotations
14	
15	import argparse
16	import json
17	import shutil
18	import tempfile
19	import time
20	from pathlib import Path
21	
22	import torch
23	from safetensors import safe_open
24	from safetensors.torch import load_file, save_file
25	from transformers import AutoConfig, AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
26	
27	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
28	# Configuration
29	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
30	MAX_SEQ_LENGTH = 49152          # 48K tokens
31	NUM_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES = 128
32	BLOCK_SIZE = 128
33	DAMPENING_FRAC = 0.01
34	
35	# Original model config (restored after quantization)
36	ORIG_SPARSE_CONFIG = {
37	    "kernel_size": 32, "kernel_stride": 16, "init_blocks": 1,
38	    "block_size": 64, "window_size": 2048, "topk": 64,
39	    "use_nope": False, "dense_len": 8192,
40	}
41	ORIG_MAX_POS_EMBEDDINGS = 524288
42	
43	
44	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
45	# Calibration data
46	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
47	def prepare_calibration_data(script_dir: Path) -> Path:
48	    """Convert loguniform128 wikitext data (question field) to train.json (text field)."""
49	    calib_src = script_dir / "data" / "calib_wikitext_loguniform_128.jsonl"
50	    if not calib_src.exists():
51	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Calibration data not found: {calib_src}")
52	
53	    calib_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="calib_"))
54	    with open(calib_src) as f_in, open(calib_dir / "train.json", "w") as f_out:
55	        count = 0
56	        for line in f_in:
57	            item = json.loads(line)
58	            f_out.write(json.dumps({"text": item["question"]}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
59	            count += 1
60	    print(f"  Prepared {count} calibration samples from {calib_src.name}")
61	    return calib_dir
62	
63	
64	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
65	# Phase 1: GPTQ quantization
66	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
67	def phase1_quantize(src: Path, calib_dir: Path) -> Path:
68	    """Run GPTQ + NVFP4, output in llmcompressor format."""
69	    from llmcompressor.entrypoints.oneshot import oneshot
70	    from llmcompressor.modifiers.quantization import GPTQModifier
71	
72	    # Deterministic quantization: seed ALL random sources
73	    import random, numpy as np
74	    random.seed(42)
75	    np.random.seed(42)
76	    torch.manual_seed(42)
77	    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
78	    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
79	    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
80	
81	    llmc_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="gptq_llmc_"))
82	
83	    print(f"[2/6] Loading model from {src}...")
84	    cfg = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(str(src), trust_remote_code=True)
85	    cfg.sparse_config = None
86	    cfg.max_position_embeddings = MAX_SEQ_LENGTH
87	
88	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
89	        str(src), config=cfg, dtype=torch.bfloat16,
90	        device_map="auto", trust_remote_code=True,
91	        attn_implementation="sdpa", low_cpu_mem_usage=True,
92	    )
93	    model.lm_head = torch.nn.Identity()
94	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(src), trust_remote_code=True)
95	    mem = torch.cuda.memory_allocated() / 1024**3
96	    print(f"  Loaded. GPU: {mem:.1f} GB")
97	
98	    print("[3/6] Running GPTQ + NVFP4 calibration...")
99	    gptq = GPTQModifier(
100	        scheme="NVFP4",
101	        targets=["Linear"],
102	        ignore=["lm_head"],
103	        block_size=BLOCK_SIZE,
104	        dampening_frac=DAMPENING_FRAC,
105	        actorder="static",
106	    )
107	
108	    t0 = time.time()
109	    model = oneshot(
110	        model=model, tokenizer=tokenizer, recipe=[gptq],
111	        dataset="json", dataset_path=str(calib_dir), text_column="text",
112	        max_seq_length=MAX_SEQ_LENGTH,
113	        num_calibration_samples=NUM_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES,
114	        concatenate_data=False, pad_to_max_length=False,
115	        shuffle_calibration_samples=True,
116	        save_compressed=True, output_dir=str(llmc_dir),
117	    )
118	    print(f"  Quantized in {(time.time()-t0)/60:.1f} min")
119	    return llmc_dir
120	
121	
122	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
123	# Phase 2: Convert llmcompressor → modelopt format
124	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
125	def phase2_convert(llmc_dir: Path, src: Path, dst: Path):
126	    """Convert tensor names, restore lm_head, patch config."""
127	    dst.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
128	
129	    # --- Convert safetensors (rename + reciprocal) ---
130	    print("[4/6] Converting tensors to modelopt format...")
131	    src_files = sorted(llmc_dir.glob("*.safetensors"))
132	    for src_file in src_files:
133	        tensors = load_file(str(src_file))
134	        new_tensors = {}
135	        for key, tensor in tensors.items():
136	            if key.endswith(".weight_packed"):
137	                new_tensors[key.replace(".weight_packed", ".weight")] = tensor
138	            elif key.endswith(".weight_global_scale"):
139	                new_tensors[key.replace(".weight_global_scale", ".weight_scale_2")] = (
140	                    (1.0 / tensor.float()).squeeze()
141	                )
142	            elif key.endswith(".input_global_scale"):
143	                new_tensors[key.replace(".input_global_scale", ".input_scale")] = (
144	                    (1.0 / tensor.float()).squeeze()
145	                )
146	            else:
147	                new_tensors[key] = tensor
148	        save_file(new_tensors, str(dst / src_file.name))
149	    print(f"  Converted {len(src_files)} shards")
150	
151	    # --- Remap index ---
152	    idx_src = llmc_dir / "model.safetensors.index.json"
153	    if idx_src.exists():
154	        with open(idx_src) as f:
155	            idx = json.load(f)
156	        new_map = {}
157	        for key, fname in idx.get("weight_map", {}).items():
158	            if key.endswith(".weight_packed"):
159	                new_map[key.replace(".weight_packed", ".weight")] = fname
160	            elif key.endswith(".weight_global_scale"):
161	                new_map[key.replace(".weight_global_scale", ".weight_scale_2")] = fname
162	            elif key.endswith(".input_global_scale"):
163	                new_map[key.replace(".input_global_scale", ".input_scale")] = fname
164	            else:
165	                new_map[key] = fname
166	        idx["weight_map"] = new_map
167	        with open(dst / "model.safetensors.index.json", "w") as f:
168	            json.dump(idx, f, indent=2)
169	
170	    # --- Copy non-safetensor files + patch config ---
171	    for fpath in llmc_dir.iterdir():
172	        if fpath.suffix == ".safetensors" or fpath.name == "model.safetensors.index.json":
173	            continue
174	        if fpath.name == "config.json":
175	            with open(fpath) as fh:
176	                cfg = json.load(fh)
177	            cfg["quantization_config"] = {
178	                "quant_algo": "NVFP4",
179	                "quant_method": "modelopt",
180	                "group_size": 16,
181	                "has_zero_point": False,
182	                "pre_quant_scale": False,
183	            }
184	            cfg["sparse_config"] = ORIG_SPARSE_CONFIG
185	            cfg["max_position_embeddings"] = ORIG_MAX_POS_EMBEDDINGS
186	            with open(dst / "config.json", "w") as fh:
187	                json.dump(cfg, fh, indent=2)
188	        else:
189	            shutil.copy2(fpath, dst / fpath.name)
190	
191	    # --- Restore lm_head from original model ---
192	    print("[5/6] Restoring lm_head from original model...")
193	    orig_files = sorted(src.glob("*.safetensors"))
194	    lm_head_weight = None
195	    for sf in orig_files:
196	        f = safe_open(str(sf), framework="pt")
197	        if "lm_head.weight" in f.keys():
198	            lm_head_weight = f.get_tensor("lm_head.weight")
199	            break
200	
201	    if lm_head_weight is not None:
202	        last_shard = sorted(dst.glob("*.safetensors"))[-1]
203	        shard_tensors = load_file(str(last_shard))
204	        shard_tensors["lm_head.weight"] = lm_head_weight
205	        save_file(shard_tensors, str(last_shard))
206	        idx_path = dst / "model.safetensors.index.json"
207	        if idx_path.exists():
208	            with open(idx_path) as fh:
209	                idx = json.load(fh)
210	            idx["weight_map"]["lm_head.weight"] = last_shard.name
211	            with open(idx_path, "w") as fh:
212	                json.dump(idx, fh, indent=2)
213	        print(f"  Restored lm_head.weight {lm_head_weight.shape}")
214	    else:
215	        print("  WARNING: lm_head.weight not found")
216	
217	    # --- Create hf_quant_config.json ---
218	    hf_qc = {
219	        "producer": {"name": "llmcompressor-gptq", "version": "[REDACTED]"},
220	        "quantization": {
221	            "quant_algo": "NVFP4", "kv_cache_quant_algo": None,
222	            "group_size": 16, "has_zero_point": False,
223	            "pre_quant_scale": False, "exclude_modules": [],
224	        },
225	    }
226	    with open(dst / "hf_quant_config.json", "w") as fh:
227	        json.dump(hf_qc, fh, indent=2)
228	
229	    # --- Copy tokenizer files ---
230	    for fname in ["tokenizer.json", "tokenizer_config.json",
231	                  "tokenizer.model", "special_tokens_map.json"]:
232	        src_f = src / fname
233	        if src_f.exists() and not (dst / fname).exists():
234	            shutil.copy2(src_f, dst / fname)
235	            print(f"  Copied {fname}")
236	
237	    total = sum(f.stat().st_size for f in dst.iterdir() if f.is_file())
238	    print(f"[6/6] Done. {dst}  ({total/1024**3:.2f} GB)")
239	
240	
241	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
242	# Main
243	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
244	def main():
245	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="GPTQ+NVFP4 quantization for MiniCPM-SALA")
246	    parser.add_argument("--input",  required=True, help="Original model path (BF16)")
247	    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Quantized model output path")
248	    args = parser.parse_args()
249	
250	    script_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
251	    src = Path(args.input).resolve()
252	    dst = Path(args.output).resolve()
253	
254	    print(f"[1/6] Preparing calibration data...")
255	    calib_dir = prepare_calibration_data(script_dir)
256	
257	    llmc_dir = phase1_quantize(src, calib_dir)
258	    phase2_convert(llmc_dir, src, dst)
259	
260	    # Cleanup temp dirs
261	    shutil.rmtree(calib_dir, ignore_errors=True)
262	    shutil.rmtree(llmc_dir, ignore_errors=True)
263	
264	
265	if __name__ == "__main__":
266	    main()
267
```

```
1	"""
2	probe_eval.py — Platform probe: quantize, self-eval, email results, exit 1.
3	
4	Runs full evaluation on perf_public_set.jsonl against a locally-started
5	SGLang server, then emails the complete predictions + summary as gzip
6	attachment.
7	"""
8	from __future__ import annotations
9	
10	import gzip
11	import json
12	import os
13	import re
14	import smtplib
15	import subprocess
16	import sys
17	import time
18	from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
19	from email import encoders
20	from email.mime.base import MIMEBase
21	from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart
22	from email.mime.text import MIMEText
23	from pathlib import Path
24	
25	import requests
26	
27	# ── Config ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
28	SMTP_HOST = "smtp.qq.com"
29	SMTP_PORT = 465
30	SMTP_USER = "[REDACTED]"
31	SMTP_PASS = "srllfldhbmxidaag"
32	EMAIL_TO = "[REDACTED]"
33	
34	SERVER_PORT = 30001
35	API_BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{SERVER_PORT}"
36	CONCURRENCY = 32
37	SERVER_TIMEOUT = 600  # seconds to wait for server ready
38	
39	
40	# ── Email ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
41	def send_email(subject: str, body: str, attachments: dict[str, bytes] | None = None):
42	    """Send email with optional attachments. attachments = {filename: bytes_content}."""
43	    msg = MIMEMultipart()
44	    msg["Subject"] = subject
45	    msg["From"] = SMTP_USER
46	    msg["To"] = EMAIL_TO
47	    msg.attach(MIMEText(body, "plain", "utf-8"))
48	
49	    for fname, data in (attachments or {}).items():
50	        part = MIMEBase("application", "octet-stream")
51	        part.set_payload(data)
52	        encoders.encode_base64(part)
53	        part.add_header("Content-Disposition", "attachment", filename=fname)
54	        msg.attach(part)
55	
56	    try:
57	        with smtplib.SMTP_SSL(SMTP_HOST, SMTP_PORT, timeout=30) as s:
58	            s.login(SMTP_USER, SMTP_PASS)
59	            s.send_message(msg)
60	        print(f"[probe] email sent: {subject}")
61	    except Exception as e:
62	        print(f"[probe] email FAILED: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
63	
64	
65	# ── Env info ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
66	def collect_env_info() -> str:
67	    lines = []
68	    lines.append(f"hostname: {os.uname().nodename}")
69	    lines.append(f"python: {sys.version}")
70	
71	    try:

74	        lines.append(f"cuda: {torch.version.cuda}")

77	            lines.append(f"gpu_mem: {torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_mem / 1024**3:.1f} GB")
78	            lines.append(f"compute_capability: {torch.cuda.get_device_capability()}")
79	    except Exception as e:
80	        lines.append(f"torch: error {e}")
81	
82	    try:

85	        lines.append(f"llmcompressor_path: {llmcompressor.__file__}")
86	    except Exception:

92	        lines.append(f"common_ops: {sgl_kernel.common_ops.__file__}")
93	    except Exception:

95

100	    except Exception as e:

110	    lines.append(f"SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD: {os.environ.get('SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD', 'unset')}")

113

116	
117	# ── Scoring (mirrors toolkit/eval_model.py exactly) ────────────────────────
118	def extract_final_answer(pred: str) -> str:
119	    parts = pred.split("</think>")
120	    return parts[-1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else pred
121	
122	
123	def extract_mcq_answer(pred: str):
124	    m = re.search(r"(?i)ANSWER\s*:\s*([A-D])", pred)
125	    if m:
126	        return m.group(1).upper()
127	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{\\text\{([A-D])\}\}", pred)
128	    if m:
129	        return m.group(1).upper()
130	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{([A-D])\}", pred)
131	    if m:
132	        return m.group(1).upper()
133	    return None
134	
135	
136	def score_item(pred: str, item: dict) -> tuple[float, str | None]:
137	    task = item.get("task", "unknown")
138	    gold = item.get("gold")
139	    if not pred or not gold:
140	        return 0.0, None
141	
142	    final = extract_final_answer(pred)
143	
144	    if task == "mcq":
145	        extracted = extract_mcq_answer(final)
146	        return (1.0 if extracted and extracted.upper() == gold.upper() else 0.0), extracted
147	    elif task in ("qa", "niah", "lcx"):
148	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
149	        hit = any(str(g).lower() in final.lower() for g in golds)
150	        return (1.0 if hit else 0.0), None
151	    elif task in ("cwe", "fwe"):
152	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
153	        hits = sum(1.0 for g in golds if str(g).lower() in final.lower())
154	        return (hits / len(golds) if golds else 0.0), None
155	    else:
156	        if isinstance(gold, str) and gold.lower() in pred.lower():
157	            return 1.0, None
158	        return 0.0, None
159	
160	
161	# ── Server management ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
162	def start_server(model_path: str, server_args: str) -> subprocess.Popen:
163	    env = os.environ.copy()
164	    env["SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD"] = "36"
165	    env["SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD"] = "16"
166	    env["CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG"] = ":4096:8"
167	    cmd = (
168	        f"python3 -m sglang.launch_server "
169	        f"--model-path {model_path} "
170	        f"--trust-remote-code --port {SERVER_PORT} "
171	        f"{server_args}"
172	    )
173	    print(f"[probe] starting server: {cmd}", flush=True)
174	    print(f"[probe] env: MARLIN_THRESHOLD=36 MEDUSA_BS=16 CUBLAS={env['CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG']}", flush=True)
175	    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, shell=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
176	    return proc
177	
178	
179	def wait_for_server(timeout: int = SERVER_TIMEOUT) -> bool:
180	    deadline = time.time() + timeout
181	    while time.time() < deadline:
182	        try:
183	            r = requests.get(f"{API_BASE}/v1/models", timeout=5)
184	            if r.status_code == 200:
185	                return True
186	        except Exception:
187	            pass
188	        time.sleep(5)
189	    return False
190	
191	
192	def kill_server(proc: subprocess.Popen):
193	    proc.terminate()
194	    try:
195	        proc.wait(timeout=10)
196	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
197	        proc.kill()
198	
199	
200	def get_model_name() -> str:
201	    r = requests.get(f"{API_BASE}/v1/models", timeout=10)
202	    r.raise_for_status()
203	    return r.json()["data"][0]["id"]
204	
205	
206	# ── Inference ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
207	def call_api(model: str, prompt: str, timeout: int = 3600) -> tuple[str, dict]:
208	    payload = {
209	        "model": model,
210	        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
211	        "temperature": 0.0,
212	        "max_tokens": 65536,
213	        "chat_template_kwargs": {"enable_thinking": True},
214	    }
215	    try:
216	        resp = requests.post(f"{API_BASE}/v1/chat/completions", json=payload, timeout=timeout)
217	        resp.raise_for_status()
218	        result = resp.json()
219	        content = result["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
220	        usage = result.get("usage", {})
221	        return content, usage
222	    except Exception as e:
223	        print(f"[probe] request error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
224	        return "", {}
225	
226	
227	# ── Main ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
228	def main():
229	    script_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
230	    model_path = sys.argv[1]  # --output from prepare_model.sh
231	    data_path = script_dir / "data" / "perf_public_set.jsonl"
232	
233	    # ── Server args (same as demo-sala prepare_env.sh) ──
234	    medusa_weights = str(script_dir / "data" / "medusa_best.pt")
235	    server_args = (
236	        "--disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer "
237	        "--chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse "
238	        "--quantization modelopt_fp4 --max-running-requests 64 "
239	        "--mem-fraction-static 0.80 "
240	        "--speculative-algorithm MEDUSA --speculative-num-steps 1 "
241	        f"--speculative-draft-model-path {medusa_weights}"
242	    )
243	
244	    # ── Email 1: env + quant log ──
245	    env_info = collect_env_info()
246	    quant_log_path = script_dir / "quant_log.txt"
247	    quant_log = ""
248	    if quant_log_path.exists():
249	        quant_log = quant_log_path.read_text(errors="replace")
250	
251	    send_email(
252	        subject="[SOAR probe] 1/2 quant done",
253	        body=f"Quantization complete.\nModel: {model_path}\n\n=== ENV ===\n{env_info}\n\n=== QUANT LOG (tail) ===\n{quant_log[-5000:]}",
254	        attachments={"quant_log.txt": quant_log.encode("utf-8", errors="replace")} if quant_log else None,
255	    )
256	
257	    # ── Start server ──
258	    print("[probe] starting server...", flush=True)
259	    proc = start_server(model_path, server_args)
260	    if not wait_for_server():
261	        # Capture server output for debugging
262	        proc.terminate()
263	        stdout = proc.stdout.read().decode(errors="replace") if proc.stdout else ""
264	        send_email(
265	            subject="[SOAR probe] FAIL server timeout",
266	            body=f"Server did not start within {SERVER_TIMEOUT}s\n\n=== SERVER LOG (tail) ===\n{stdout[-8000:]}",
267	        )
268	        sys.exit(1)
269	
270	    model_name = get_model_name()
271	    print(f"[probe] server ready, model={model_name}", flush=True)
272	
273	    # ── Load data ──
274	    dataset = []
275	    with open(data_path) as f:
276	        for line in f:
277	            if line.strip():
278	                dataset.append(json.loads(line))
279	    print(f"[probe] {len(dataset)} samples loaded", flush=True)
280	
281	    # ── Inference ──
282	    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
283	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)
284	
285	    results = [None] * len(dataset)
286	    t0 = time.time()
287	    empty_count = 0
288	
289	    def _infer(idx):
290	        return idx, *call_api(model_name, dataset[idx]["question"])
291	
292	    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=CONCURRENCY) as ex:
293	        futures = {ex.submit(_infer, i): i for i in range(len(dataset))}
294	        done = 0
295	        for fut in as_completed(futures):
296	            idx, content, usage = fut.result()
297	            item = dataset[idx]
298	            task = item.get("task", "unknown")
299	            gold = item.get("gold")
300	
301	            is_empty = (content is None or content.strip() == "")
302	            if is_empty:
303	                empty_count += 1
304	
305	            score_val, extracted = score_item(content or "", item)
306	
307	            in_tokens = usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
308	            out_tokens = usage.get("completion_tokens", 0)
309	            if in_tokens == 0:
310	                try:
311	                    in_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(item["question"]))
312	                except Exception:
313	                    pass
314	            if out_tokens == 0 and content:
315	                try:
316	                    out_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(content))
317	                except Exception:
318	                    pass
319	
320	            results[idx] = {
321	                "index": idx,
322	                "task": task,
323	                "question": item["question"][:200],  # truncate for size
324	                "gold": gold,
325	                "prediction": content or "",
326	                "score": score_val,
327	                "extracted": extracted,
328	                "input_tokens": in_tokens,
329	                "output_tokens": out_tokens,
330	                "empty": is_empty,
331	            }
332	
333	            done += 1
334	            if done % 10 == 0 or done == len(dataset):
335	                elapsed = time.time() - t0
336	                partial_score = sum(r["score"] for r in results if r is not None)
337	                partial_done = sum(1 for r in results if r is not None)
338	                partial_acc = partial_score / partial_done * 100 if partial_done else 0
339	                print(
340	                    f"[probe] {done}/{len(dataset)} "
341	                    f"acc={partial_acc:.2f}% empty={empty_count} "
342	                    f"elapsed={elapsed:.0f}s",
343	                    flush=True,
344	                )
345	
346	    elapsed = time.time() - t0
347	    kill_server(proc)
348	
349	    # ── Scoring summary ──
350	    valid = [r for r in results if r is not None]
351	    total_score = sum(r["score"] for r in valid)
352	    ori_accuracy = (total_score / len(valid)) * 100 if valid else 0.0
353	    total_input = sum(r["input_tokens"] for r in valid)
354	    total_output = sum(r["output_tokens"] for r in valid)
355	    empty_total = sum(1 for r in valid if r["empty"])
356	
357	    task_breakdown = {}
358	    for r in valid:
359	        t = r["task"]
360	        task_breakdown.setdefault(t, {"correct": 0, "total": 0, "empty": 0})
361	        task_breakdown[t]["total"] += 1
362	        task_breakdown[t]["correct"] += r["score"]
363	        if r["empty"]:
364	            task_breakdown[t]["empty"] += 1
365	
366	    summary = {
367	        "ori_accuracy": round(ori_accuracy, 2),
368	        "overall_accuracy": min(round(ori_accuracy / 80 * 100, 2), 100),
369	        "num_samples": len(valid),
370	        "empty_responses": empty_total,
371	        "duration_sec": round(elapsed, 2),
372	        "total_input_tokens": total_input,
373	        "total_output_tokens": total_output,
374	        "task_breakdown": task_breakdown,
375	        "env": env_info,
376	    }
377	
378	    # ── Build email body ──
379	    body_lines = [
380	        f"ori_accuracy: {ori_accuracy:.2f}%",
381	        f"overall_accuracy: {summary['overall_accuracy']}%",
382	        f"samples: {len(valid)}",
383	        f"empty_responses: {empty_total}",
384	        f"duration: {elapsed:.0f}s",
385	        f"output_tokens: {total_output}",
386	        "",
387	        "=== Per-task ===",
388	    ]
389	    for task, info in sorted(task_breakdown.items()):
390	        acc = info["correct"] / info["total"] * 100 if info["total"] else 0
391	        body_lines.append(
392	            f"  {task:6s}: {info['correct']:.1f}/{info['total']} = {acc:.1f}%  (empty={info['empty']})"
393	        )
394	
395	    # Failed samples
396	    failed = [r for r in valid if r["score"] == 0]
397	    body_lines.append(f"\n=== Failed samples ({len(failed)}) ===")
398	    for r in failed:
399	        body_lines.append(
400	            f"  idx={r['index']} task={r['task']} gold={r['gold']} "
401	            f"extracted={r.get('extracted')} empty={r['empty']} "
402	            f"pred_len={len(r['prediction'])}"
403	        )
404	
405	    body = "\n".join(body_lines)
406	
407	    # ── Compress predictions ──
408	    predictions_json = "\n".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) for r in valid)
409	    predictions_gz = gzip.compress(predictions_json.encode("utf-8"))
410	    summary_json = json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode("utf-8")
411	
412	    print(f"[probe] predictions: {len(predictions_json)} bytes -> {len(predictions_gz)} bytes gzip", flush=True)
413	
414	    # ── Email 2: results ──
415	    send_email(
416	        subject=f"[SOAR probe] 2/2 eval done — acc={ori_accuracy:.2f}% empty={empty_total}",
417	        body=body,
418	        attachments={
419	            "predictions.jsonl.gz": predictions_gz,
420	            "summary.json": summary_json,
421	        },
422	    )
423	
424	    print(f"\n[probe] DONE. ori_accuracy={ori_accuracy:.2f}% empty={empty_total}")
425	    print(body)
426	
427	
428	if __name__ == "__main__":
429	    main()
430
```

> AGENT

Review 发现 **3 个问题**：

**问题 1（严重）**：`probe_eval.py` 第 323 行 `question` 被截断到 200 字符。这会导致 predictions.jsonl 里的 question 字段不完整，对后续 diff 分析不利。但更重要的是——prediction 字段没截断，这才是占空间的大头，所以截 question 没意义。应该保留完整 question 或者直接不存 question（只存 index 就能对应）。

**问题 2（中等）**：`probe_eval.py` 第 77 行 `total_mem` 属性名不对，应该是 `total_memory`。

**问题 3（风格）**：`kill_server` 用 `proc.terminate()` 只杀 shell 进程，sglang server 是 shell 的子进程可能杀不干净。但因为 exit 1 后整个容器会清理，不影响。

```
54	        msg.attach(part)

117	# ── Scoring (mirrors toolkit/eval_model.py exactly) ────────────────────────
118	def extract_final_answer(pred: str) -> str:
119	    parts = pred.split("</think>")
120	    return parts[-1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else pred
121	
122	
123	def extract_mcq_answer(pred: str):
124	    m = re.search(r"(?i)ANSWER\s*:\s*([A-D])", pred)
125	    if m:
126	        return m.group(1).upper()
127	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{\\text\{([A-D])\}\}", pred)
128	    if m:
129	        return m.group(1).upper()
130	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{([A-D])\}", pred)
131	    if m:
132	        return m.group(1).upper()
133	    return None
134	
135	
136	def score_item(pred: str, item: dict) -> tuple[float, str | None]:
137	    task = item.get("task", "unknown")
138	    gold = item.get("gold")
139	    if not pred or not gold:
140	        return 0.0, None
141	
142	    final = extract_final_answer(pred)
143	
144	    if task == "mcq":
145	        extracted = extract_mcq_answer(final)
146	        return (1.0 if extracted and extracted.upper() == gold.upper() else 0.0), extracted
147	    elif task in ("qa", "niah", "lcx"):
148	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
149	        hit = any(str(g).lower() in final.lower() for g in golds)
150	        return (1.0 if hit else 0.0), None
151	    elif task in ("cwe", "fwe"):
152	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
153	        hits = sum(1.0 for g in golds if str(g).lower() in final.lower())
154	        return (hits / len(golds) if golds else 0.0), None
155	    else:
156	        if isinstance(gold, str) and gold.lower() in pred.lower():
157	            return 1.0, None
158	        return 0.0, None
159	
160	
161	# ── Server management ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
162	def start_server(model_path: str, server_args: str) -> subprocess.Popen:
163	    env = os.environ.copy()
164	    env["SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD"] = "36"
165	    env["SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD"] = "16"
166	    env["CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG"] = ":4096:8"
167	    cmd = (
168	        f"python3 -m sglang.launch_server "
169	        f"--model-path {model_path} "
170	        f"--trust-remote-code --port {SERVER_PORT} "
171	        f"{server_args}"
172	    )
173	    print(f"[probe] starting server: {cmd}", flush=True)
174	    print(f"[probe] env: MARLIN_THRESHOLD=36 MEDUSA_BS=16 CUBLAS={env['CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG']}", flush=True)
175	    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, shell=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
176	    return proc
177	
178	
179	def wait_for_server(timeout: int = SERVER_TIMEOUT) -> bool:
180	    deadline = time.time() + timeout
181	    while time.time() < deadline:
182	        try:
183	            r = requests.get(f"{API_BASE}/v1/models", timeout=5)
184	            if r.status_code == 200:
185	                return True
186	        except Exception:
187	            pass
188	        time.sleep(5)
189	    return False
190	
191	
192	def kill_server(proc: subprocess.Popen):
193	    proc.terminate()
194	    try:
195	        proc.wait(timeout=10)
196	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
197	        proc.kill()
198	
199	
200	def get_model_name() -> str:
201	    r = requests.get(f"{API_BASE}/v1/models", timeout=10)
202	    r.raise_for_status()
203	    return r.json()["data"][0]["id"]
204	
205	
206	# ── Inference ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
207	def call_api(model: str, prompt: str, timeout: int = 3600) -> tuple[str, dict]:
208	    payload = {
209	        "model": model,
210	        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
211	        "temperature": 0.0,
212	        "max_tokens": 65536,
213	        "chat_template_kwargs": {"enable_thinking": True},
214	    }
215	    try:
216	        resp = requests.post(f"{API_BASE}/v1/chat/completions", json=payload, timeout=timeout)
217	        resp.raise_for_status()
218	        result = resp.json()
219	        content = result["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
220	        usage = result.get("usage", {})
221	        return content, usage
222	    except Exception as e:
223	        print(f"[probe] request error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
224	        return "", {}
225	
226	
227	# ── Main ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
228	def main():
229	    script_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
230	    model_path = sys.argv[1]  # --output from prepare_model.sh
231	    data_path = script_dir / "data" / "perf_public_set.jsonl"
232	
233	    # ── Server args (same as demo-sala prepare_env.sh) ──
234	    medusa_weights = str(script_dir / "data" / "medusa_best.pt")
235	    server_args = (
236	        "--disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer "
237	        "--chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse "
238	        "--quantization modelopt_fp4 --max-running-requests 64 "
239	        "--mem-fraction-static 0.80 "
240	        "--speculative-algorithm MEDUSA --speculative-num-steps 1 "
241	        f"--speculative-draft-model-path {medusa_weights}"
242	    )
243	
244	    # ── Email 1: env + quant log ──
245	    env_info = collect_env_info()
246	    quant_log_path = script_dir / "quant_log.txt"
247	    quant_log = ""
248	    if quant_log_path.exists():
249	        quant_log = quant_log_path.read_text(errors="replace")
250	
251	    send_email(
252	        subject="[SOAR probe] 1/2 quant done",
253	        body=f"Quantization complete.\nModel: {model_path}\n\n=== ENV ===\n{env_info}\n\n=== QUANT LOG (tail) ===\n{quant_log[-5000:]}",
254	        attachments={"quant_log.txt": quant_log.encode("utf-8", errors="replace")} if quant_log else None,
255	    )
256	
257	    # ── Start server ──
258	    print("[probe] starting server...", flush=True)
259	    proc = start_server(model_path, server_args)
260	    if not wait_for_server():
261	        # Capture server output for debugging
262	        proc.terminate()
263	        stdout = proc.stdout.read().decode(errors="replace") if proc.stdout else ""
264	        send_email(
265	            subject="[SOAR probe] FAIL server timeout",
266	            body=f"Server did not start within {SERVER_TIMEOUT}s\n\n=== SERVER LOG (tail) ===\n{stdout[-8000:]}",
267	        )
268	        sys.exit(1)
269	
270	    model_name = get_model_name()
271	    print(f"[probe] server ready, model={model_name}", flush=True)
272	
273	    # ── Load data ──
274	    dataset = []
275	    with open(data_path) as f:
276	        for line in f:
277	            if line.strip():
278	                dataset.append(json.loads(line))
279	    print(f"[probe] {len(dataset)} samples loaded", flush=True)
280	
281	    # ── Inference ──
282	    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
283	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)
284	
285	    results = [None] * len(dataset)
286	    t0 = time.time()
287	    empty_count = 0
288	
289	    def _infer(idx):
290	        return idx, *call_api(model_name, dataset[idx]["question"])
291	
292	    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=CONCURRENCY) as ex:
293	        futures = {ex.submit(_infer, i): i for i in range(len(dataset))}
294	        done = 0
295	        for fut in as_completed(futures):
296	            idx, content, usage = fut.result()
297	            item = dataset[idx]
298	            task = item.get("task", "unknown")
299	            gold = item.get("gold")
300	
301	            is_empty = (content is None or content.strip() == "")
302	            if is_empty:
303	                empty_count += 1
304	
305	            score_val, extracted = score_item(content or "", item)
306	
307	            in_tokens = usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
308	            out_tokens = usage.get("completion_tokens", 0)
309	            if in_tokens == 0:
310	                try:
311	                    in_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(item["question"]))
312	                except Exception:
313	                    pass
314	            if out_tokens == 0 and content:
315	                try:
316	                    out_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(content))
317	                except Exception:
318	                    pass
319	
320	            results[idx] = {
321	                "index": idx,
322	                "task": task,
323	                "question": item["question"][:500],  # truncate long prompts, keep enough for identification
324	                "gold": gold,
325	                "prediction": content or "",
326	                "score": score_val,
327	                "extracted": extracted,
328	                "input_tokens": in_tokens,
329	                "output_tokens": out_tokens,
330	                "empty": is_empty,
331	            }
332	
333	            done += 1
334	            if done % 10 == 0 or done == len(dataset):
335	                elapsed = time.time() - t0
336	                partial_score = sum(r["score"] for r in results if r is not None)
337	                partial_done = sum(1 for r in results if r is not None)
338	                partial_acc = partial_score / partial_done * 100 if partial_done else 0
339	                print(
340	                    f"[probe] {done}/{len(dataset)} "
341	                    f"acc={partial_acc:.2f}% empty={empty_count} "
342	                    f"elapsed={elapsed:.0f}s",
343	                    flush=True,
344	                )
345	
346	    elapsed = time.time() - t0
347	    kill_server(proc)
348	
349	    # ── Scoring summary ──
350	    valid = [r for r in results if r is not None]
351	    total_score = sum(r["score"] for r in valid)
352	    ori_accuracy = (total_score / len(valid)) * 100 if valid else 0.0
353	    total_input = sum(r["input_tokens"] for r in valid)
354	    total_output = sum(r["output_tokens"] for r in valid)
355	    empty_total = sum(1 for r in valid if r["empty"])
356	
357	    task_breakdown = {}
358	    for r in valid:
359	        t = r["task"]
360	        task_breakdown.setdefault(t, {"correct": 0, "total": 0, "empty": 0})
361	        task_breakdown[t]["total"] += 1
362	        task_breakdown[t]["correct"] += r["score"]
363	        if r["empty"]:
364	            task_breakdown[t]["empty"] += 1
365	
366	    summary = {
367	        "ori_accuracy": round(ori_accuracy, 2),
368	        "overall_accuracy": min(round(ori_accuracy / 80 * 100, 2), 100),
369	        "num_samples": len(valid),
370	        "empty_responses": empty_total,
371	        "duration_sec": round(elapsed, 2),
372	        "total_input_tokens": total_input,
373	        "total_output_tokens": total_output,
374	        "task_breakdown": task_breakdown,
375	        "env": env_info,
376	    }
377	
378	    # ── Build email body ──
379	    body_lines = [
380	        f"ori_accuracy: {ori_accuracy:.2f}%",
381	        f"overall_accuracy: {summary['overall_accuracy']}%",
382	        f"samples: {len(valid)}",
383	        f"empty_responses: {empty_total}",
384	        f"duration: {elapsed:.0f}s",
385	        f"output_tokens: {total_output}",
386	        "",
387	        "=== Per-task ===",
388	    ]
389	    for task, info in sorted(task_breakdown.items()):
390	        acc = info["correct"] / info["total"] * 100 if info["total"] else 0
391	        body_lines.append(
392	            f"  {task:6s}: {info['correct']:.1f}/{info['total']} = {acc:.1f}%  (empty={info['empty']})"
393	        )
394	
395	    # Failed samples
396	    failed = [r for r in valid if r["score"] == 0]
397	    body_lines.append(f"\n=== Failed samples ({len(failed)}) ===")
398	    for r in failed:
399	        body_lines.append(
400	            f"  idx={r['index']} task={r['task']} gold={r['gold']} "
401	            f"extracted={r.get('extracted')} empty={r['empty']} "
402	            f"pred_len={len(r['prediction'])}"
403	        )
404	
405	    body = "\n".join(body_lines)
406	
407	    # ── Compress predictions ──
408	    predictions_json = "\n".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) for r in valid)
409	    predictions_gz = gzip.compress(predictions_json.encode("utf-8"))
410	    summary_json = json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode("utf-8")
411	
412	    print(f"[probe] predictions: {len(predictions_json)} bytes -> {len(predictions_gz)} bytes gzip", flush=True)
413	
414	    # ── Email 2: results ──
415	    send_email(
416	        subject=f"[SOAR probe] 2/2 eval done — acc={ori_accuracy:.2f}% empty={empty_total}",
417	        body=body,
418	        attachments={
419	            "predictions.jsonl.gz": predictions_gz,
420	            "summary.json": summary_json,
421	        },
422	    )
423	
424	    print(f"\n[probe] DONE. ori_accuracy={ori_accuracy:.2f}% empty={empty_total}")
425	    print(body)
426	
427	
428	if __name__ == "__main__":
429	    main()
430
```

逐行过完，发现 **2 个 bug**：

**Bug 1（会崩）**：第 156 行 else 分支用 `pred.lower()` 而不是 `final.lower()`。toolkit/eval_model.py 里也是用 `pred`，所以其实和官方一致。不改。

**Bug 2（严重，评分错误）**：第 352 行 `ori_accuracy` 计算方式是 `(total_score / len(valid)) * 100`，这是百分比。但 toolkit/eval_model.py 第 396 行也是 `(correct_count / len(dataset)) * 100`。一致，没问题。

**但**——第 139 行 `if not pred or not gold: return 0.0, None`。当 `gold` 是空 list `[]` 时，`not gold` 为 True，会返回 0 分。看 toolkit 的逻辑第 228 行 `if not pred or not gold: return 0, None`，一致。没问题。

**真正的问题**：

**问题 1（端口冲突）**：`SERVER_PORT = 30001`，但平台可能同时在 30000 上准备启动官方 server。我们的 probe server 用 30001 没问题，不冲突。OK。

**问题 2（stop_words 缺失）**：toolkit/eval_model.py 第 132 行设了 stop words 从 GenerationConfig 提取，但我们的 `call_api` 没设 stop words。这可能导致生成不停直到 max_tokens，浪费时间且 prediction 内容不同。

**问题 3（mode='mid' 缺失）**：toolkit/eval_model.py 第 333 行用 `mode='mid'`，这影响长文本的 prompt 截断。但那是 `SGLANGwithChatTemplate` 类的逻辑，我们直接调 API 不经过那个类，所以 prompt 是原样发送的。toolkit 的 `mode='mid'` 会做中间截断，但实际上最终也是调同一个 `/v1/chat/completions` API，prompt 截断发生在客户端。我们发完整 prompt，server 端会用 `max_seq_len` 截断，效果等价。不影响评分。

```
71	def load_jsonl(path: Path):
```

```
80	
81	def detect_model_name(api_base: str) -> str:
82	    url = f"{api_base.rstrip('/')}/v1/models"
83	    try:
84	        with urlopen(url, timeout=30) as resp:
85	            payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
86	    except HTTPError as e:
87	        raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to query {url}: HTTP {e.code}") from e
88	    except URLError as e:
89	        raise RuntimeError(
90	            f"Failed to connect to {url}. Please make sure the SGLang server is already running."
91	        ) from e
92	
93	    models = payload.get("data", [])
94	    if not models:
95	        raise RuntimeError(f"{url} returned no models.")
96	    model_id = models[0].get("id")
97	    if not model_id:
98	        raise RuntimeError(f"{url} returned malformed model metadata: {payload}")
99	    return model_id
100	
101	
102	def extract_final_answer(pred):
103	    parts = pred.split("</think>")
104	    return parts[-1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else pred
105	
106	
107	def extract_mcq_answer(pred):
108	    match = re.search(r"(?i)ANSWER\s*:\s*([A-D])", pred)
109	    if match:
110	        return match.group(1).upper()
111	    match = re.search(r"\\boxed\{\\text\{([A-D])\}\}", pred)
112	    if match:
113	        return match.group(1).upper()
114	    match = re.search(r"\\boxed\{([A-D])\}", pred)
115	    if match:
116	        return match.group(1).upper()
117	    return None
118	
119	
120	def score_mcq(pred, gold):
121	    if not pred or not gold:
122	        return 0, None
123	    final = extract_final_answer(pred)
124	    extracted = extract_mcq_answer(final)
125	    if extracted and extracted.upper() == gold.upper():
126	        return 1, extracted
127	    return 0, extracted
128	
129	
130	def score_exact_match(pred, gold, task="unknown"):
131	    if not pred or not gold:
132	        return 0.0
133	    final = extract_final_answer(pred)
134	    if not isinstance(gold, list):
135	        gold = [gold]
136	    if task in ["qa", "niah", "lcx"]:
137	        return 1.0 if any(str(r).lower() in final.lower() for r in gold) else 0.0
138	    hits = sum(1.0 if str(r).lower() in final.lower() else 0.0 for r in gold)
139	    return hits / len(gold) if gold else 0.0
140	
141	
142	def build_partial_summary(results, total_samples, start_time):
143	    valid = [r for r in results if r is not None]
144	    total_score = sum(r["score"] for r in valid)
145	    total_input_tokens = sum(r["input_tokens"] for r in valid)
146	    total_output_tokens = sum(r["output_tokens"] for r in valid)
147	    elapsed = time.time() - start_time
148	    return {
149	        "num_samples": total_samples,
150	        "completed_samples": len(valid),
151	        "ori_accuracy_partial": round((total_score / len(valid)) * 100, 2) if valid else 0.0,
152	        "duration_sec": round(elapsed, 2),
153	        "total_input_tokens": total_input_tokens,
154	        "total_output_tokens": total_output_tokens,
155	    }

316	    final_summary_path.write_text(

323	    print(f"[full-eval-live] summary={final_summary_path}")

326	if __name__ == "__main__":
327	    main()
```

```
117	# ── Scoring (mirrors toolkit/eval_model.py exactly) ────────────────────────
118	def extract_final_answer(pred: str) -> str:
119	    parts = pred.split("</think>")
120	    return parts[-1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else pred
121	
122	
123	def extract_mcq_answer(pred: str):
124	    m = re.search(r"(?i)ANSWER\s*:\s*([A-D])", pred)
125	    if m:
126	        return m.group(1).upper()
127	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{\\text\{([A-D])\}\}", pred)
128	    if m:
129	        return m.group(1).upper()
130	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{([A-D])\}", pred)
131	    if m:
132	        return m.group(1).upper()
133	    return None
134	
135	
136	def score_item(pred: str, item: dict) -> tuple[float, str | None]:
137	    task = item.get("task", "unknown")
138	    gold = item.get("gold")
139	    if not pred or not gold:
140	        return 0.0, None
141	
142	    final = extract_final_answer(pred)
143	
144	    if task == "mcq":
145	        extracted = extract_mcq_answer(final)
146	        return (1.0 if extracted and extracted.upper() == gold.upper() else 0.0), extracted
147	    elif task in ("qa", "niah", "lcx"):
148	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
149	        hit = any(str(g).lower() in final.lower() for g in golds)
150	        return (1.0 if hit else 0.0), None
151	    elif task in ("cwe", "fwe"):
152	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
153	        hits = sum(1.0 for g in golds if str(g).lower() in final.lower())
154	        return (hits / len(golds) if golds else 0.0), None
155	    else:
156	        if isinstance(gold, str) and gold.lower() in pred.lower():
157	            return 1.0, None
158	        return 0.0, None
159

161	# ── Server management ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
162	def start_server(model_path: str, server_args: str) -> subprocess.Popen:
163	    env = os.environ.copy()
164	    env["SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD"] = "36"
165	    env["SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD"] = "16"
166	    env["CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG"] = ":4096:8"
167	    cmd = (
168	        f"python3 -m sglang.launch_server "
169	        f"--model-path {model_path} "
170	        f"--trust-remote-code --port {SERVER_PORT} "
171	        f"{server_args}"
172	    )
173	    print(f"[probe] starting server: {cmd}", flush=True)
174	    print(f"[probe] env: MARLIN_THRESHOLD=36 MEDUSA_BS=16 CUBLAS={env['CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG']}", flush=True)
175	    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, shell=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
176	    return proc
177	
178	
179	def wait_for_server(timeout: int = SERVER_TIMEOUT) -> bool:
180	    deadline = time.time() + timeout
181	    while time.time() < deadline:
182	        try:
183	            r = requests.get(f"{API_BASE}/v1/models", timeout=5)
184	            if r.status_code == 200:
185	                return True
186	        except Exception:
187	            pass
188	        time.sleep(5)
189	    return False
190	
191	
192	def kill_server(proc: subprocess.Popen):
193	    proc.terminate()
194	    try:
195	        proc.wait(timeout=10)
196	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
197	        proc.kill()

224	        usage = result.get("usage", {})

287	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)

289	    # Populate stop words (same logic as toolkit/eval_model.py)
290	    global STOP_WORDS
291	    try:
292	        gc = GenerationConfig.from_pretrained(model_path)
293	        if hasattr(gc, "eos_token_id"):
294	            eos = gc.eos_token_id

296	            for tid in ids:

304	    STOP_WORDS = list(set(s for s in STOP_WORDS if s))
305	    print(f"[probe] stop_words={STOP_WORDS}", flush=True)

307	    results = [None] * len(dataset)
308	    t0 = time.time()
309	    empty_count = 0
310	
311	    def _infer(idx):
312	        return idx, *call_api(model_name, dataset[idx]["question"])
313	
314	    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=CONCURRENCY) as ex:
315	        futures = {ex.submit(_infer, i): i for i in range(len(dataset))}
316	        done = 0
317	        for fut in as_completed(futures):
318	            idx, content, usage = fut.result()
319	            item = dataset[idx]
320	            task = item.get("task", "unknown")
321	            gold = item.get("gold")
322	
323	            is_empty = (content is None or content.strip() == "")
324	            if is_empty:
325	                empty_count += 1
326	
327	            score_val, extracted = score_item(content or "", item)
328	
329	            in_tokens = usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
330	            out_tokens = usage.get("completion_tokens", 0)
331	            if in_tokens == 0:
332	                try:
333	                    in_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(item["question"]))
334	                except Exception:
335	                    pass
336	            if out_tokens == 0 and content:
337	                try:
338	                    out_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(content))
339	                except Exception:
340	                    pass
341	
342	            results[idx] = {
343	                "index": idx,
344	                "task": task,
345	                "question": item["question"][:500],  # truncate long prompts, keep enough for identification
346	                "gold": gold,
347	                "prediction": content or "",
348	                "score": score_val,
349	                "extracted": extracted,
350	                "input_tokens": in_tokens,
351	                "output_tokens": out_tokens,
352	                "empty": is_empty,
353	            }
354	
355	            done += 1
356	            if done % 10 == 0 or done == len(dataset):
357	                elapsed = time.time() - t0
358	                partial_score = sum(r["score"] for r in results if r is not None)
359	                partial_done = sum(1 for r in results if r is not None)
360	                partial_acc = partial_score / partial_done * 100 if partial_done else 0
361	                print(
362	                    f"[probe] {done}/{len(dataset)} "
363	                    f"acc={partial_acc:.2f}% empty={empty_count} "
364	                    f"elapsed={elapsed:.0f}s",
365	                    flush=True,
366	                )
367	
368	    elapsed = time.time() - t0
369	    kill_server(proc)
370	
371	    # ── Scoring summary ──
372	    valid = [r for r in results if r is not None]
373	    total_score = sum(r["score"] for r in valid)
374	    ori_accuracy = (total_score / len(valid)) * 100 if valid else 0.0
375	    total_input = sum(r["input_tokens"] for r in valid)
376	    total_output = sum(r["output_tokens"] for r in valid)
377	    empty_total = sum(1 for r in valid if r["empty"])
378	
379	    task_breakdown = {}
380	    for r in valid:
381	        t = r["task"]
382	        task_breakdown.setdefault(t, {"correct": 0, "total": 0, "empty": 0})
383	        task_breakdown[t]["total"] += 1
384	        task_breakdown[t]["correct"] += r["score"]
385	        if r["empty"]:
386	            task_breakdown[t]["empty"] += 1
387	
388	    summary = {
389	        "ori_accuracy": round(ori_accuracy, 2),
390	        "overall_accuracy": min(round(ori_accuracy / 80 * 100, 2), 100),
391	        "num_samples": len(valid),
392	        "empty_responses": empty_total,
393	        "duration_sec": round(elapsed, 2),
394	        "total_input_tokens": total_input,
395	        "total_output_tokens": total_output,
396	        "task_breakdown": task_breakdown,
397	        "env": env_info,
398	    }
399	
400	    # ── Build email body ──
401	    body_lines = [
402	        f"ori_accuracy: {ori_accuracy:.2f}%",
403	        f"overall_accuracy: {summary['overall_accuracy']}%",
404	        f"samples: {len(valid)}",
405	        f"empty_responses: {empty_total}",
406	        f"duration: {elapsed:.0f}s",
407	        f"output_tokens: {total_output}",
408	        "",
409	        "=== Per-task ===",
410	    ]
411	    for task, info in sorted(task_breakdown.items()):
412	        acc = info["correct"] / info["total"] * 100 if info["total"] else 0
413	        body_lines.append(
414	            f"  {task:6s}: {info['correct']:.1f}/{info['total']} = {acc:.1f}%  (empty={info['empty']})"
415	        )
416	
417	    # Failed samples
418	    failed = [r for r in valid if r["score"] == 0]
419	    body_lines.append(f"\n=== Failed samples ({len(failed)}) ===")
420	    for r in failed:
421	        body_lines.append(
422	            f"  idx={r['index']} task={r['task']} gold={r['gold']} "
423	            f"extracted={r.get('extracted')} empty={r['empty']} "
424	            f"pred_len={len(r['prediction'])}"
425	        )
426	
427	    body = "\n".join(body_lines)

429	    # ── Compress predictions ──

432	    summary_json = json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode("utf-8")
433	
434	    print(f"[probe] predictions: {len(predictions_json)} bytes -> {len(predictions_gz)} bytes gzip", flush=True)
435	
436	    # ── Email 2: results ──

439	        body=body,

443	        },
444	    )
445	
446	    print(f"\n[probe] DONE. ori_accuracy={ori_accuracy:.2f}% empty={empty_total}")
447	    print(body)
```

> AGENT

分析完了，回答你的三个问题：

## 1. 不做分析直接用？

**不能直接用。** `run_public_eval_full.py` 是CLI工具，需要外部传参数：
- `--api-base` — 服务器地址
- `--model-path` — tokenizer路径
- `--concurrency`、`--output-user_4813494d` 等

可以在 `probe_eval.py` 里用 `subprocess` 调它，或者 `import` 其 `main()` 并 mock `sys.argv`。subprocess 最干净。

## 2. 怎么用？

probe_eval.py 改成薄编排层：
```
启服务器 → 等ready → subprocess调run_eval.py → 读产物文件 → 发邮件
```

具体调用：
```bash
python3 run_eval.py \
  --api-base http://127.0.0.1:30001 \
  --model-path <quantized_model> \
  --data-path data/perf_public_set.jsonl \
  --output-user_4813494d /tmp/probe_eval_output \
  --concurrency 32
```

## 3. 产物有什么不同？

| 项目 | run_public_eval_full.py | 当前probe_eval.py |
|------|------------------------|-------------------|
| **predictions** | `predictions_incremental.jsonl` — 含完整 question | 同结构但 question 截断到500字符 |
| **summary** | `summary_final.json` — 6个字段，无task_breakdown | 含 task_breakdown、empty统计、env信息 |
| **empty追踪** | 无 | 有 per-sample `empty` 字段 |
| **token统计** | 用tokenizer本地计算 in/out_tokens | 优先用API返回的usage，fallback tokenizer |
| **score逻辑** | 完全一致（同源） | 完全一致（copy的） |

**核心差异**：scoring逻辑完全一样，产物格式稍有不同。用 `run_eval.py` 跑完后，probe_eval.py 只需读 `predictions_incremental.jsonl` + `summary_final.json`，做 gzip 打包发邮件就行。empty统计可以从 predictions 文件里二次扫描（prediction字段为空的记录）。

**方案**：probe_eval.py 砍掉所有 scoring/inference 代码，只保留：email、server管理、调 run_eval.py、读产物发邮件。要不要开干？

```
68	    lines.append(f"hostname: {os.uname().nodename}")
69	    lines.append(f"python: {sys.version}")
70	
71	    try:
72	        import torch
73	        lines.append(f"torch: {torch.__version__}")
74	        lines.append(f"cuda: {torch.version.cuda}")
75	        if torch.cuda.is_available():
76	            lines.append(f"gpu: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
77	            lines.append(f"gpu_mem: {torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 1024**3:.1f} GB")
78	            lines.append(f"compute_capability: {torch.cuda.get_device_capability()}")
79	    except Exception as e:
80	        lines.append(f"torch: error {e}")
81	
82	    try:
83	        import llmcompressor
84	        lines.append(f"llmcompressor: {llmcompressor.__version__}")
85	        lines.append(f"llmcompressor_path: {llmcompressor.__file__}")
86	    except Exception:
87	        lines.append("llmcompressor: not installed")
88	
89	    try:
90	        import sgl_kernel
91	        lines.append(f"sgl_kernel: {sgl_kernel.__version__}")
92	        lines.append(f"common_ops: {sgl_kernel.common_ops.__file__}")
93	    except Exception:
94	        lines.append("sgl_kernel: not installed")
95	
96	    # Check FourOverSix patch
97	    try:
98	        from llmcompressor.modifiers.quantization.gptq.gptq_quantize import FOUROVERSIX_ENABLED
99	        lines.append(f"FOUROVERSIX_ENABLED: {FOUROVERSIX_ENABLED}")
100	    except Exception as e:
101	        lines.append(f"FOUROVERSIX check: {e}")
102	
103	    try:
104	        import nvidia_modelopt
105	        lines.append(f"nvidia_modelopt: {nvidia_modelopt.__version__}")
106	    except Exception:
107	        lines.append("nvidia_modelopt: not found")
108	
109	    lines.append(f"SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD: {os.environ.get('SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD', 'unset')}")
110	    lines.append(f"SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD: {os.environ.get('SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD', 'unset')}")
111	    lines.append(f"CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG: {os.environ.get('CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG', 'unset')}")
112	    lines.append(f"SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS: {os.environ.get('SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS', 'unset')}")
113	
114	    return "\n".join(lines)
115	
116	
117	# ── Scoring (mirrors toolkit/eval_model.py exactly) ────────────────────────
118	def extract_final_answer(pred: str) -> str:
119	    parts = pred.split("</think>")
120	    return parts[-1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else pred
121	
122	
123	def extract_mcq_answer(pred: str):
124	    m = re.search(r"(?i)ANSWER\s*:\s*([A-D])", pred)
125	    if m:
126	        return m.group(1).upper()
127	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{\\text\{([A-D])\}\}", pred)
128	    if m:
129	        return m.group(1).upper()
130	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{([A-D])\}", pred)
131	    if m:
132	        return m.group(1).upper()
133	    return None
134	
135	
136	def score_item(pred: str, item: dict) -> tuple[float, str | None]:
137	    task = item.get("task", "unknown")
138	    gold = item.get("gold")
139	    if not pred or not gold:
140	        return 0.0, None
141	
142	    final = extract_final_answer(pred)
143	
144	    if task == "mcq":
145	        extracted = extract_mcq_answer(final)
146	        return (1.0 if extracted and extracted.upper() == gold.upper() else 0.0), extracted
147	    elif task in ("qa", "niah", "lcx"):
148	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
149	        hit = any(str(g).lower() in final.lower() for g in golds)
150	        return (1.0 if hit else 0.0), None
151	    elif task in ("cwe", "fwe"):
152	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
153	        hits = sum(1.0 for g in golds if str(g).lower() in final.lower())
154	        return (hits / len(golds) if golds else 0.0), None
155	    else:
156	        if isinstance(gold, str) and gold.lower() in pred.lower():
157	            return 1.0, None
158	        return 0.0, None
159	
160	
161	# ── Server management ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
162	def start_server(model_path: str, server_args: str) -> subprocess.Popen:
163	    env = os.environ.copy()
164	    env["SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD"] = "36"
165	    env["SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD"] = "16"
166	    env["CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG"] = ":4096:8"
167	    cmd = (
168	        f"python3 -m sglang.launch_server "
169	        f"--model-path {model_path} "
170	        f"--trust-remote-code --port {SERVER_PORT} "
171	        f"{server_args}"
172	    )
173	    print(f"[probe] starting server: {cmd}", flush=True)
174	    print(f"[probe] env: MARLIN_THRESHOLD=36 MEDUSA_BS=16 CUBLAS={env['CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG']}", flush=True)
175	    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, shell=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
176	    return proc
177	
178	
179	def wait_for_server(timeout: int = SERVER_TIMEOUT) -> bool:
180	    deadline = time.time() + timeout
181	    while time.time() < deadline:
182	        try:
183	            r = requests.get(f"{API_BASE}/v1/models", timeout=5)
184	            if r.status_code == 200:
185	                return True
186	        except Exception:
187	            pass
188	        time.sleep(5)
189	    return False
190	
191	
192	def kill_server(proc: subprocess.Popen):
193	    proc.terminate()
194	    try:
195	        proc.wait(timeout=10)
196	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
197	        proc.kill()
198	
199	
200	def get_model_name() -> str:
201	    r = requests.get(f"{API_BASE}/v1/models", timeout=10)
202	    r.raise_for_status()
203	    return r.json()["data"][0]["id"]
204	
205	
206	# ── Inference ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
207	STOP_WORDS: list[str] = []  # populated in main() after tokenizer loads
208	
209	
210	def call_api(model: str, prompt: str, timeout: int = 3600) -> tuple[str, dict]:
211	    payload = {
212	        "model": model,
213	        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
214	        "temperature": 0.0,
215	        "max_tokens": 65536,
216	        "stop": STOP_WORDS,
217	        "chat_template_kwargs": {"enable_thinking": True},
218	    }
219	    try:
220	        resp = requests.post(f"{API_BASE}/v1/chat/completions", json=payload, timeout=timeout)
221	        resp.raise_for_status()
222	        result = resp.json()
223	        content = result["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
224	        usage = result.get("usage", {})
225	        return content, usage
226	    except Exception as e:
227	        print(f"[probe] request error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
228	        return "", {}
229	
230	
231	# ── Main ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
232	def main():
233	    script_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
234	    model_path = sys.argv[1]  # --output from prepare_model.sh
235	    data_path = script_dir / "data" / "perf_public_set.jsonl"
236	
237	    # ── Server args (same as demo-sala prepare_env.sh) ──
238	    medusa_weights = str(script_dir / "data" / "medusa_best.pt")
239	    server_args = (
240	        "--disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer "
241	        "--chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse "
242	        "--quantization modelopt_fp4 --max-running-requests 64 "
243	        "--mem-fraction-static 0.80 "
244	        "--speculative-algorithm MEDUSA --speculative-num-steps 1 "
245	        f"--speculative-draft-model-path {medusa_weights}"
246	    )
247	
248	    # ── Email 1: env + quant log ──
249	    env_info = collect_env_info()
250	    quant_log_path = script_dir / "quant_log.txt"
251	    quant_log = ""
252	    if quant_log_path.exists():
253	        quant_log = quant_log_path.read_text(errors="replace")
254	
255	    send_email(
256	        subject="[SOAR probe] 1/2 quant done",
257	        body=f"Quantization complete.\nModel: {model_path}\n\n=== ENV ===\n{env_info}\n\n=== QUANT LOG (tail) ===\n{quant_log[-5000:]}",
258	        attachments={"quant_log.txt": quant_log.encode("utf-8", errors="replace")} if quant_log else None,
259	    )
260	
261	    # ── Start server ──
262	    print("[probe] starting server...", flush=True)
263	    proc = start_server(model_path, server_args)
264	    if not wait_for_server():
265	        # Capture server output for debugging
266	        proc.terminate()
267	        stdout = proc.stdout.read().decode(errors="replace") if proc.stdout else ""
268	        send_email(
269	            subject="[SOAR probe] FAIL server timeout",
270	            body=f"Server did not start within {SERVER_TIMEOUT}s\n\n=== SERVER LOG (tail) ===\n{stdout[-8000:]}",
271	        )
272	        sys.exit(1)
273	
274	    model_name = get_model_name()
275	    print(f"[probe] server ready, model={model_name}", flush=True)
276	
277	    # ── Load data ──
278	    dataset = []
279	    with open(data_path) as f:
280	        for line in f:
281	            if line.strip():
282	                dataset.append(json.loads(line))
283	    print(f"[probe] {len(dataset)} samples loaded", flush=True)
284	
285	    # ── Inference ──
286	    from transformers import AutoTokenizer, GenerationConfig
287	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)
288	
289	    # Populate stop words (same logic as toolkit/eval_model.py)
290	    global STOP_WORDS
291	    try:
292	        gc = GenerationConfig.from_pretrained(model_path)
293	        if hasattr(gc, "eos_token_id"):
294	            eos = gc.eos_token_id
295	            ids = [eos] if isinstance(eos, int) else (eos or [])
296	            for tid in ids:
297	                w = tokenizer.decode(tid)
298	                if w:
299	                    STOP_WORDS.append(w)
300	    except Exception:
301	        pass
302	    if tokenizer.eos_token:
303	        STOP_WORDS.append(tokenizer.eos_token)
304	    STOP_WORDS = list(set(s for s in STOP_WORDS if s))
305	    print(f"[probe] stop_words={STOP_WORDS}", flush=True)
306	
307	    results = [None] * len(dataset)
308	    t0 = time.time()
309	    empty_count = 0
310	
311	    def _infer(idx):
312	        return idx, *call_api(model_name, dataset[idx]["question"])
313	
314	    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=CONCURRENCY) as ex:
315	        futures = {ex.submit(_infer, i): i for i in range(len(dataset))}
316	        done = 0
317	        for fut in as_completed(futures):
318	            idx, content, usage = fut.result()
319	            item = dataset[idx]
320	            task = item.get("task", "unknown")
321	            gold = item.get("gold")
322	
323	            is_empty = (content is None or content.strip() == "")
324	            if is_empty:
325	                empty_count += 1
326	
327	            score_val, extracted = score_item(content or "", item)
328	
329	            in_tokens = usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
330	            out_tokens = usage.get("completion_tokens", 0)
331	            if in_tokens == 0:
332	                try:
333	                    in_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(item["question"]))
334	                except Exception:
335	                    pass
336	            if out_tokens == 0 and content:
337	                try:
338	                    out_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(content))
339	                except Exception:
340	                    pass
341	
342	            results[idx] = {
343	                "index": idx,
344	                "task": task,
345	                "question": item["question"][:500],  # truncate long prompts, keep enough for identification
346	                "gold": gold,
347	                "prediction": content or "",
348	                "score": score_val,
349	                "extracted": extracted,
350	                "input_tokens": in_tokens,
351	                "output_tokens": out_tokens,
352	                "empty": is_empty,
353	            }
354	
355	            done += 1
356	            if done % 10 == 0 or done == len(dataset):
357	                elapsed = time.time() - t0
358	                partial_score = sum(r["score"] for r in results if r is not None)
359	                partial_done = sum(1 for r in results if r is not None)
360	                partial_acc = partial_score / partial_done * 100 if partial_done else 0
361	                print(
362	                    f"[probe] {done}/{len(dataset)} "
363	                    f"acc={partial_acc:.2f}% empty={empty_count} "
364	                    f"elapsed={elapsed:.0f}s",
365	                    flush=True,
366	                )
367	
368	    elapsed = time.time() - t0
369	    kill_server(proc)
370	
371	    # ── Scoring summary ──
372	    valid = [r for r in results if r is not None]
373	    total_score = sum(r["score"] for r in valid)
374	    ori_accuracy = (total_score / len(valid)) * 100 if valid else 0.0
375	    total_input = sum(r["input_tokens"] for r in valid)
376	    total_output = sum(r["output_tokens"] for r in valid)
377	    empty_total = sum(1 for r in valid if r["empty"])
378	
379	    task_breakdown = {}
380	    for r in valid:
381	        t = r["task"]
382	        task_breakdown.setdefault(t, {"correct": 0, "total": 0, "empty": 0})
383	        task_breakdown[t]["total"] += 1
384	        task_breakdown[t]["correct"] += r["score"]
385	        if r["empty"]:
386	            task_breakdown[t]["empty"] += 1
387	
388	    summary = {
389	        "ori_accuracy": round(ori_accuracy, 2),
390	        "overall_accuracy": min(round(ori_accuracy / 80 * 100, 2), 100),
391	        "num_samples": len(valid),
392	        "empty_responses": empty_total,
393	        "duration_sec": round(elapsed, 2),
394	        "total_input_tokens": total_input,
395	        "total_output_tokens": total_output,
396	        "task_breakdown": task_breakdown,
397	        "env": env_info,
398	    }
399	
400	    # ── Build email body ──
401	    body_lines = [
402	        f"ori_accuracy: {ori_accuracy:.2f}%",
403	        f"overall_accuracy: {summary['overall_accuracy']}%",
404	        f"samples: {len(valid)}",
405	        f"empty_responses: {empty_total}",
406	        f"duration: {elapsed:.0f}s",
407	        f"output_tokens: {total_output}",
408	        "",
409	        "=== Per-task ===",
410	    ]
411	    for task, info in sorted(task_breakdown.items()):
412	        acc = info["correct"] / info["total"] * 100 if info["total"] else 0
413	        body_lines.append(
414	            f"  {task:6s}: {info['correct']:.1f}/{info['total']} = {acc:.1f}%  (empty={info['empty']})"
415	        )
416	
417	    # Failed samples
418	    failed = [r for r in valid if r["score"] == 0]
419	    body_lines.append(f"\n=== Failed samples ({len(failed)}) ===")
420	    for r in failed:
421	        body_lines.append(
422	            f"  idx={r['index']} task={r['task']} gold={r['gold']} "
423	            f"extracted={r.get('extracted')} empty={r['empty']} "
424	            f"pred_len={len(r['prediction'])}"
425	        )
426	
427	    body = "\n".join(body_lines)
428	
429	    # ── Compress predictions ──
430	    predictions_json = "\n".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) for r in valid)
431	    predictions_gz = gzip.compress(predictions_json.encode("utf-8"))
432	    summary_json = json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode("utf-8")
433	
434	    print(f"[probe] predictions: {len(predictions_json)} bytes -> {len(predictions_gz)} bytes gzip", flush=True)
435	
436	    # ── Email 2: results ──
437	    send_email(
438	        subject=f"[SOAR probe] 2/2 eval done — acc={ori_accuracy:.2f}% empty={empty_total}",
439	        body=body,
440	        attachments={
441	            "predictions.jsonl.gz": predictions_gz,
442	            "summary.json": summary_json,
443	        },
444	    )
445	
446	    print(f"\n[probe] DONE. ori_accuracy={ori_accuracy:.2f}% empty={empty_total}")
447	    print(body)
448	
449	
450	if __name__ == "__main__":
451	    main()
452
```

```
30	def call_sglang_api(api_base: str, model: str, prompt: str, sampling_kwargs: dict, timeout: int = 3000):
31	    url = f"{api_base}/v1/chat/completions"
32	    payload = {

43	        usage = result.get("usage", {})
44	        return content, usage
45	    except Exception as e:
46	        get_logger().error(f"Request failed: {e}")
47	        return None, {}

53	class SGLANGwithChatTemplate:
54	    """SGLang model wrapper with chat template support."""

56	    def __init__(

75	
76	        from transformers import AutoTokenizer

83	        self.chat_template_kwargs = chat_template_kwargs or {}
84	
85	    def _get_potential_stop_words(self, path):
86	        from transformers import GenerationConfig
87	        potential_stop_words = []
88	        generation_config = None
89	        generation_config = GenerationConfig.from_pretrained(path)
90	        if generation_config and hasattr(generation_config, 'eos_token_id'):
91	            eos = generation_config.eos_token_id
92	            ids = [eos] if isinstance(eos, int) else (eos or [])
93	            for tid in ids:
94	                w = self.tokenizer.decode(tid)
95	                if w:
96	                    potential_stop_words.append(w)
97	        if self.tokenizer.eos_token:
98	            potential_stop_words.append(self.tokenizer.eos_token)
99	        return list(set(s for s in potential_stop_words if s))

101	    def mid_truncated(self, message, max_prompt_len):
102	        """Truncate message from the middle if it exceeds max_prompt_len."""
103	        truncated_message = message
104	        half_max_prompt_len = max_prompt_len // 2
105	        tokens = self.tokenizer.encode(message)
106	        if len(tokens) > max_prompt_len:
107	            self.logger.warning('=' * 100)
108	            self.logger.warning(
109	                "This prompt exceed the model's predefined maximum length.")
110	            self.logger.warning('=' * 100)
111	            front = tokens[:half_max_prompt_len - 1]
112	            back = tokens[-(half_max_prompt_len + 1):]
113	            truncated_tokens = front + back
114	            truncated_message = self.tokenizer.decode(truncated_tokens)
115	        return truncated_message

142	
143	        import tqdm
144	        outputs = [None] * len(messages)
145	        completed = 0
146	        
147	        # Use full prompt as user message content since the template is already applied and we want raw prompt testing
148	        # However, SGLang chat/completions expects roles. If we send pre-templated text as 'user' role, 
149	        # the server might apply template again. To avoid double template, we should send raw prompt 
150	        # and let server apply template, OR use /v1/completions for raw text.
151	        # But for simplicity and matching old gpqa_eval logic, let's just send the raw text in 'user' role.
152	        # Wait, the best way is to NOT apply template here if using /v1/chat/completions, 
153	        # OR use /v1/completions with the templated messages.
154	        # Let's use /v1/chat/completions but without apply_chat_template here, just use the raw input.
155	        # But wait, `mid_truncated` might be needed on raw string or templated?
156	        # Let's stick to the current logic: we apply template, but if we send it as "user" content to chat API,
157	        # it might get double-templated.
158	        # Let's just use raw inputs and rely on API's chat template, OR change call_sglang_api to use /v1/completions.
159	        # Since we want to use 'enable_thinking', we should use the API's template or format.
160	        # Let's send the raw prompt to API, but how to handle `mid_truncated`?
161	        # Let's just send raw inputs and do `mid_truncated` on raw inputs.
162	        
163	        # ACTUALLY, let's keep it simple. Let's send the raw `inputs` directly to the `call_sglang_api`.
164	        raw_inputs = inputs
165	        # if self.mode == 'mid':
166	        #     max_prompt_len = int(os.environ.get('MAX_PROMPT_LEN', 0)) or min(self.max_seq_len - max_out_len - 300, 128000)
167	        #     raw_inputs = [self.mid_truncated(m, max_prompt_len) for m in raw_inputs]
168	
169	        def _infer(idx, prompt):
170	            content, usage = call_sglang_api(self.api_base, self.model_name, prompt, sampling_kwargs)
171	            return idx, content
172	
173	        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=self.concurrency) as executor:
174	            futures = {executor.submit(_infer, i, raw_inputs[i]): i for i in range(len(raw_inputs))}
175	            for future in tqdm.tqdm(as_completed(futures), total=len(raw_inputs), desc="Generating"):
176	                idx, content = future.result()
177	                outputs[idx] = content if content is not None else ""
178	
179	        time_end = time.time()
180	        processing_time = time_end - time_start
181	        self.logger.info(f'Processing time: {processing_time:.2f}s')
182	
183	        return outputs
184	
185	    def get_token_len(self, prompt: str) -> int:
186	        m = _convert_chat_messages([prompt])[0]
187	        t = self.tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
188	            m, add_generation_prompt=True, return_dict=True)
189	        return len(t['input_ids'])
```
