191	# ==========================================
192	# Main Test/Inference Script
193	# ==========================================
194	
195	def parse_args():
196	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
197	    parser.add_argument('--model_path', type=str, default='openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA', help="Model Path")
198	    parser.add_argument('--api_base', type=str, default='http://127.0.0.1:30000', help="SGLang API base URL")
199	    parser.add_argument('--model_name', type=str, default=None, help="Model name for API requests. Auto-detected if not set.")
200	    parser.add_argument('--data_path', type=str, default='data/public_set.jsonl')
201	    parser.add_argument('--max_seq_len', type=int, default=262144)
202	    parser.add_argument('--concurrency', type=int, default=8, help="Number of concurrent API requests")
203	    parser.add_argument('--num_samples', type=int, default=None, help="Number of samples to test")
204	    parser.add_argument('--verbose', action='store_true', help="Print per-sample details")
205	    return parser.parse_args()
206	
207	def extract_final_answer(pred):
208	    """Extract content after </think> tag, falling back to full prediction."""
209	    parts = pred.split('</think>')
210	    return parts[-1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else pred
211	
212	def extract_mcq_answer(pred):
213	    """Extract MCQ answer letter from prediction, supporting multiple formats."""
214	    # Format 1: ANSWER: X (standard)
215	    match = re.search(r'(?i)ANSWER\s*:\s*([A-D])', pred)
216	    if match:
217	        return match.group(1).upper()
218	    # Format 2: \boxed{\text{X}} or \boxed{X} (LaTeX)
219	    match = re.search(r'\\boxed\{\\text\{([A-D])\}\}', pred)
220	    if match:
221	        return match.group(1).upper()
222	    match = re.search(r'\\boxed\{([A-D])\}', pred)
223	    if match:
224	        return match.group(1).upper()
225	    return None
226	
227	def score_mcq(pred, gold):
228	    if not pred or not gold: return 0, None
229	    final = extract_final_answer(pred)
230	    extracted = extract_mcq_answer(final)
231	    if extracted and extracted.upper() == gold.upper():
232	        return 1, extracted
233	    return 0, extracted
234	
235	def score_exact_match(pred, gold, task="unknown"):
236	    if not pred or not gold: return 0
237	    final = extract_final_answer(pred)
238	    if not isinstance(gold, list): gold = [gold]
239	    
240	    # 针对长文本任务评分的瑕疵修复：
241	    # 如果是 QA 类型的任务，gold 列表通常是同一答案的不同表述（同义词），只要命中任意一个就算满分 1。
242	    # 如果是 CWE/FWE 类型的任务，gold 列表是必须全部提取出来的多个关键词，则算覆盖率。
243	    if task in ['qa', 'niah', 'lcx']:
244	        # 只要包含任意一个候选答案即为完全正确
245	        hits = any(str(r).lower() in final.lower() for r in gold)
246	        return 1.0 if hits else 0.0
247	    else:
248	        # cwe, fwe 等需要提取所有目标词汇的任务
249	        hits = sum([1.0 if str(r).lower() in final.lower() else 0.0 for r in gold])
250	        return hits / len(gold) if gold else 0
251	
252	def print_json_result(record_id, user_id, task_id, state, error_msg="", acc=0.0, duration=0.0, total_tokens=0):
253	    result = {
254	        "record_id": record_id,
255	        "user_id": user_id,
256	        "task_id": task_id,
257	        "state": state,
258	        "result": {
259	            "error_msg": error_msg,
260	            "score": {
261	                "acc": acc,
262	                "duration": duration, # Current run duration
263	                "total_tokens": total_tokens
264	            },
265	            "sort_by": "acc"
266	        }
267	    }
268	    # Print a separator to help backend parsing if needed, though split by '{' logic usually handles it
269	    print("\n--- JSON RESULT START ---")
270	    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
271	    print("--- JSON RESULT END ---")
272	
273	def main():
274	    record_id = os.environ.get("RECORD_ID", "test_record")
275	    user_id = os.environ.get("USER_ID", "test_user")
276	    task_id = os.environ.get("TASK_ID", "test_task")
277	    
278	    args = parse_args()
279	    # Auto-detect model name if not set
280	    if not args.model_name:
281	        try:
282	            resp = requests.get(f"{args.api_base}/v1/models", timeout=10)
283	            resp.raise_for_status()
284	            models = resp.json()["data"]
285	            args.model_name = models[0]["id"]
286	            print(f"Auto-detected model name: {args.model_name}")
287	        except Exception as e:
288	            print(f"[ERROR] Could not auto-detect model name: {e}")
289	            print("Please specify using --model_name")
290	            sys.exit(1)
291	
292	    print(f"API Base: {args.api_base}")
293	    print(f"Model Name: {args.model_name}")
294	    if os.environ.get("DATA_PATH"):
295	        args.data_path = os.environ.get("DATA_PATH")
296	    
297	    print(f"Model Path: {args.model_path}")
298	    print(f"Data Path: {args.data_path}")
299	
300	    # Setup output directory
301	    timestamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
302	    output_dir = os.path.join("outputs", timestamp)
303	    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
304	    print(f"Saving results to {output_dir}")
305	
306	    # 1. Load Data
307	    dataset = []
308	    if os.path.exists(args.data_path):
309	        with open(args.data_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
310	            for line in f:
311	                if line.strip():
312	                    dataset.append(json.loads(line))
313	                    if args.num_samples and len(dataset) >= args.num_samples:
314	                        break
315	    else:
316	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Data file not found: {args.data_path}")
317	
318	    print(f"Testing with {len(dataset)} samples.")
319	
320	    # 2. Initialize Model Client
321	    print("Initializing model client...")
322	    model = SGLANGwithChatTemplate(
323	        path=args.model_path,
324	        api_base=args.api_base,
325	        model_name=args.model_name,
326	        max_seq_len=args.max_seq_len,
327	        concurrency=args.concurrency,
328	        generation_kwargs={
329	            "temperature": 0.0,
330	        },
331	        chat_template_kwargs={"enable_thinking": True},
332	        mode='mid',
333	    )
334	
335	    # 3. Generate
336	    inputs = [item['question'] for item in dataset]
337	    print("Generating responses...")
338	    start_time = time.time()
339	    outputs = model.generate(inputs, max_out_len=65536)
340	    end_time = time.time()
341	    print(f"\nGeneration completed in {end_time - start_time:.2f} seconds")
342	
343	    # 4. Score & Save
344	    print("\n--- Evaluation Results ---")
345	    correct_count = 0
346	    results_to_save = []
347	    tmp_output_file = os.path.join(output_dir, "_tmp_prediction.jsonl")
348	    total_input_tokens = 0
349	    total_output_tokens = 0
350	
351	    mcq_tasks = ['mcq']
352	    long_context_tasks = [
353	        'niah', 'cwe', 'fwe', 'qa', 'lcx'
354	    ]
355	
356	    for i, item in enumerate(dataset):
357	        task = item.get('task', 'unknown')
358	        pred = outputs[i]
359	        gold = item.get('gold')
360	
361	        in_len = model.get_token_len(inputs[i])
362	        out_len = model.get_token_len(pred)
363	        total_input_tokens += in_len
364	        total_output_tokens += out_len
365	
366	        score = 0
367	        extracted = None
368	
369	        if task in mcq_tasks:
370	            score, extracted = score_mcq(pred, gold)
371	        elif task in long_context_tasks:
372	            score = score_exact_match(pred, gold, task)
373	        else:
374	            if isinstance(gold, str) and gold.lower() in pred.lower():
375	                score = 1
376	
377	        correct_count += score
378	
379	        results_to_save.append({
380	            "index": i,
381	            "task": task,
382	            "question": item['question'],
383	            "gold": gold,
384	            "prediction": pred,
385	            "score": score,
386	            "extracted": extracted,
387	            "input_tokens": in_len,
388	            "output_tokens": out_len,
389	        })
390

393	            print(f"Gold: {gold}, Extracted: {extracted}, Score: {score}")
394	            print(f"Tokens: In={in_len}, Out={out_len}")
395	
396	    avg_score = (correct_count / len(dataset)) * 100 if dataset else 0
397	    duration = end_time - start_time
398	    tps = total_output_tokens / duration if duration > 0 else 0
399

401	    print(f"Total Duration: {duration:.2f} s")
402	    print(f"Total Tokens: In={total_input_tokens}, Out={total_output_tokens}")
403	    if len(dataset) > 0:
404	        print(f"Average Tokens/Sample: In={total_input_tokens/len(dataset):.1f}, Out={total_output_tokens/len(dataset):.1f}")
405	    print(f"Overall TPS (Output): {tps:.2f} tokens/s")
406

408	        for res in results_to_save:

现在我有了三份源码的完整对比。逐项报告：

## Bug 审查结果

### BUG 1（严重）：`score_item` 的 else 分支用了 `pred` 而非 `final`

**位置**：`probe_eval.py:156`
```python
if isinstance(gold, str) and gold.lower() in pred.lower():
```

`run_public_eval_full.py:266` 也一样用了 `pred`，`toolkit/eval_model.py:374` 也用了 `pred`。

**结论**：三者一致，**不是 bug**。else 分支对 unknown task 故意匹配全文（含 think 部分）。

### BUG 2（严重）：`sgl_kernel.common_ops.__file__` 可能不存在

**位置**：`probe_eval.py:92`
```python
import sgl_kernel
lines.append(f"common_ops: {sgl_kernel.common_ops.__file__}")
```

`sgl_kernel.common_ops` 可能是 `.so` 模块没有 `common_ops` 作为子属性暴露。但这在 `try/except` 里，不会崩溃。**低风险**。

### BUG 3（真正的 bug）：`kill_server` 只 terminate 了 shell，子进程可能泄漏

**位置**：`probe_eval.py:192-197`
```python
def kill_server(proc: subprocess.Popen):
    proc.terminate()
```

`start_server` 用了 `shell=True`，所以 `proc` 是 shell 进程，`proc.terminate()` 只杀 shell，**不杀 python3 sglang 子进程**。服务器可能继续跑着占用 GPU 显存。

但对 probe 场景来说，`exit 1` 之后平台会清理所有进程，**不影响结果正确性**。低风险。

### BUG 4（真正的 bug，影响精度对比）：token 统计方式不同

**位置**：`probe_eval.py:329-340` vs `toolkit/eval_model.py:361-363`

probe_eval 优先用 API 返回的 `usage.prompt_tokens/completion_tokens`，fallback 到 `tokenizer.encode()`。

而 `toolkit/eval_model.py` 用 `model.get_token_len()`，它内部走 `apply_chat_template(..., return_dict=True)["input_ids"]`。

**影响**：token 数会不同，但这只影响 token 统计，**不影响 score 计算**。probe 目的是对比精度，不影响。

### BUG 5（无 bug）：scoring 逻辑对比

逐函数对比 `probe_eval.py` vs `toolkit/eval_model.py`：

| 函数 | probe_eval | toolkit/eval_model | 一致？ |
|------|-----------|-------------------|--------|
| `extract_final_answer` | `split("</think>")[-1]` | 完全一样 | ✅ |
| `extract_mcq_answer` | 3个regex完全一样 | 完全一样 | ✅ |
| `score_mcq` / `score_item(mcq)` | `extracted.upper() == gold.upper()` | 完全一样 | ✅ |
| `score_exact_match(qa/niah/lcx)` | `any(str(g).lower() in final.lower())` | 完全一样 | ✅ |
| `score_exact_match(cwe/fwe)` | `hits/len(golds)` | 完全一样 | ✅ |

### BUG 6（真正的 bug，可能影响精度）：`call_api` 失败返回 `""` vs toolkit 返回 `None`→`""`

**位置**：`probe_eval.py:228` 异常时返回 `("", {})`

`toolkit/eval_model.py:46` 异常时返回 `(None, {})`，然后 `generate()` 里 `outputs[idx] = content if content is not None else ""`。

**结论**：最终行为一样，都变成空字符串。**一致，不是 bug。**

### BUG 7（关键差异）：`chat_template_kwargs` 传递方式

**位置**：`probe_eval.py:217`
```python
"chat_template_kwargs": {"enable_thinking": True},
```

`toolkit/eval_model.py` 的 `sampling_kwargs` **没有** `chat_template_kwargs`。它是在 `SGLANGwithChatTemplate.__init__` 里设的 `self.chat_template_kwargs`，用于本地 `apply_chat_template`，**不传给 API**。

但看 `call_sglang_api`（eval_model.py:30-44），`sampling_kwargs` 直接 update 到 payload，所以 `generation_kwargs={"temperature": 0.0}` 会进去。而 `chat_template_kwargs` **不在 sampling_kwargs 里**。

**重新看**：`eval_model.py:131-137` 的 `sampling_kwargs` 只包含 `temperature/max_tokens/stop`，再 `update(self.generation_kwargs)` 和 `update(kwargs)`。`chat_template_kwargs` 不在其中。

但 SGLang server 的 `/v1/chat/completions` 端点确实支持 `chat_template_kwargs` 参数。问题是 **toolkit 官方评测有没有传这个参数？**

让我确认。

> AGENT

看到了！`toolkit/eval_model.py:331` 设了 `chat_template_kwargs={"enable_thinking": True}`，但这只用于**本地** `apply_chat_template`（121行），**不通过 API 传给服务器**。

而 `probe_eval.py:217` 把 `chat_template_kwargs` **直接传给了 API**。SGLang 的 `/v1/chat/completions` 支持这个字段，服务器端会用它来 apply template。

**这其实是一致的**——两种方式都会让 template 以 `enable_thinking=True` 渲染。只是路径不同：
- toolkit: 本地 apply template → 发 raw prompt（但实际上 `generate()` 最后发的是 `raw_inputs`，即**不经过 template**，由 server 端 apply）
- probe: 直接发 messages + `chat_template_kwargs`，由 server apply

等等，仔细看 `eval_model.py:163`：

```python
raw_inputs = inputs  # 用原始 inputs，不用 templated messages
```

然后 `_infer` 调用 `call_sglang_api`，后者发 `{"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}`。**没有传 `chat_template_kwargs`！**

这意味着 **toolkit 官方评测不传 `enable_thinking` 给 API**。服务器会用默认 template，是否包含 thinking 取决于模型的默认 chat template。

> AGENT

关键发现在 `serving_chat.py:1107-1108`：

```python
not request.chat_template_kwargs
or request.chat_template_kwargs.get("enable_thinking", True) is True
```

**当不传 `chat_template_kwargs` 时，`not request.chat_template_kwargs` 为 True，默认开启 thinking**。所以 toolkit 不传和 probe 传 `enable_thinking: True` 的效果是一样的。**不是 bug。**

---

## 最终结论

**probe_eval.py 没有影响精度正确性的 bug。** scoring 逻辑和 toolkit 官方完全一致，API 调用参数等效。

存在的小问题：
1. **`kill_server` shell=True 泄漏**——不影响结果，平台会清理
2. **token 统计方式不同**——不影响 score
3. **question 截断到 500 字符存入 predictions**——不影响 score，但邮件附件里无法看完整 question（调试用够了）

代码可以直接用，没有会导致精度偏差的 bug。

=== 4. prepare_model.sh 内容 ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
# probe prepare_model.sh — quantize, self-eval with Medusa, email results, exit 1

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"

echo "[probe] start $(date '+%F %T')"
echo "[probe] args: $*"

# Parse --input / --output
INPUT=""
OUTPUT=""
while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
    case "$1" in
        --input)  INPUT="$2";  shift 2 ;;
        --output) OUTPUT="$2"; shift 2 ;;
        *) shift ;;
    esac
done

echo "[probe] input=$INPUT output=$OUTPUT"

if [ -z "$INPUT" ] || [ -z "$OUTPUT" ]; then
    echo "[probe] ERROR: --input and --output required" >&2
    exit 1
fi

# ── Quantize (identical to demo-sala) ──
export PYTORCH_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True
export PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True
export CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=":4096:8"

# ── Pre-quant email: env info ──
echo "[probe] sending pre-quant email..."
python3 -c "
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '${SCRIPT_DIR}')
from probe_eval import send_email, collect_env_info
env = collect_env_info()
send_email('[SOAR probe] 0/3 pre-quant env', f'About to quantize.\ninput=$INPUT\noutput=$OUTPUT\n\n=== ENV ===\n{env}')
" 2>&1 || echo "[probe] pre-quant email failed (non-fatal)"

echo "[probe] starting quantization (GPTQ+FourOverSix, 48K, loguniform128)"
python3 "${SCRIPT_DIR}/preprocess_model.py" --input "$INPUT" --output "$OUTPUT" 2>&1 | tee "${SCRIPT_DIR}/quant_log.txt"
echo "[probe] quantization done $(date '+%F %T')"

# ── Self-eval + email ──
echo "[probe] starting probe_eval..."
python3 "${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_eval.py" "$OUTPUT" 2>&1 || true

echo "[probe] probe complete $(date '+%F %T'), exiting 1 to skip official eval"
exit 1

=== 5. probe_eval.py 服务器参数 vs demo-sala prepare_env.sh ===

> AGENT

找到了。**`stdout=subprocess.PIPE`** — 经典的管道死锁。

sglang 服务器启动时输出大量日志，`PIPE` 缓冲区默认 64KB。写满后服务器进程阻塞在 write() 上，永远无法完成启动。而 probe_eval.py 在 `wait_for_server` 里轮询 `/v1/models`，**从不读 pipe**，所以永远等不到 ready。

  "new_string": "def start_server(model_path: str, server_args: str) -> subprocess.Popen:\n    env = os.environ.copy()\n    env[\"SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD\"] = \"36\"\n    env[\"SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD\"] = \"16\"\n    env[\"CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG\"] = \":4096:8\"\n    cmd = (\n        f\"python3 -m sglang.launch_server \"\n        f\"--model-path {model_path} \"\n        f\"--trust-remote-code --port {SERVER_PORT} \"\n        f\"{server_args}\"\n    )\n    print(f\"[probe] starting server: {cmd}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"[probe] env: MARLIN_THRESHOLD=36 MEDUSA_BS=16 CUBLAS={env['CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG']}\", flush=True)\n    script_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n    server_log = script_dir / \"server_log.txt\"\n    log_fh = open(server_log, \"w\")\n    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, shell=True, stdout=log_fh, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)\n    proc._log_fh = log_fh  # keep ref to close later\n    proc._log_path = server_log\n    return proc"
}

现在的流程是什么

> AGENT

平台 source `prepare_env.sh` → 执行 `prepare_model.sh --input <BF16模型> --output <目标目录>`：

1. **发邮件 0/3**（prepare_model.sh 内）— 环境信息，量化前发出，确认 probe 在跑
2. **量化**（preprocess_model.py）— GPTQ+FourOverSix, loguniform128, 48K，输出到 `$OUTPUT`，日志 tee 到 `quant_log.txt`
3. **probe_eval.py $OUTPUT** — 以下全在这里面：
   - 发邮件 **1/3** — quant_log + 环境信息
   - 启 sglang 服务器（端口 30001，stdout 写文件不走 PIPE）
   - 等 ready（轮询 `/v1/models`，超时 600s，失败发邮件 exit 1）
   - 150 样本并发评测（concurrency=32，和官方一致）
   - 发邮件 **3/3** — accuracy + task_breakdown + predictions.jsonl.gz + summary.json
   - kill 服务器
4. **exit 1** — 跳过平台官方评测

> DEVELOPER

我说的是如果是官方评测 也是等你ready？ 还是怎么做？

> AGENT

不是。官方评测（demo-sala）流程完全不同：

**demo-sala（官方评测）**
1. 平台 source `prepare_env.sh` — 设置 `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` 环境变量
2. 平台执行 `prepare_model.sh` — 只做量化，完成后 **exit 0**
3. **平台自己**用 `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` 启动 sglang 服务器
4. **平台自己**跑官方评测脚本（toolkit/eval_model.py）

**probe-sala（我们的探针）**
1. 平台 source `prepare_env.sh`
2. 平台执行 `prepare_model.sh` — 量化 + **我们自己启服务器** + **我们自己跑评测** + 发邮件
3. **exit 1** — 故意失败，跳过平台官方评测

所以官方评测时，服务器启动和评测都是平台控制的，不走我们的 probe_eval.py。`SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` 里的参数（包括 Medusa）会被平台拿去启动服务器。

2	# prepare_env.sh — sourced by platform after base image startup
3	# Do NOT set -euo pipefail here (sourced script should not exit the parent shell)
4	
5	echo "[prepare_env] start $(date '+%F %T')"
6	
7	SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
8	
9	# 1. Install our custom SGLang (replaces image built-in version)
10	uv pip install --no-deps -e "${SCRIPT_DIR}/sglang/python"
11	
12	# 2. Install nvidia-modelopt (inference) + llmcompressor (GPTQ quantization)
13	uv pip install "nvidia-modelopt==0.42.0" "llmcompressor==[REDACTED]"
14	
15	# 3. Patch llmcompressor: FourOverSix adaptive scale=4/6 selection (arXiv:2512.02010)
16	GPTQ_TARGET=[REDACTED]
17	cp "${SCRIPT_DIR}/patches/gptq_quantize_fouroversix.py" "$GPTQ_TARGET"
18	echo "[prepare_env] patched gptq_quantize.py with FourOverSix"
19	
20	# 4. Replace sgl-kernel .so with pre-built version (Marlin FP4 scale fix)
21	INSTALLED_SO="/opt/SGLang-MiniCPM-SALA/sglang_minicpm_sala_env/lib/python3.10/site-packages/sgl_kernel/sm100/common_ops.abi3.so"
22	cp "$INSTALLED_SO" "${INSTALLED_SO}.bak"
23	cp "${SCRIPT_DIR}/common_ops.abi3.so" "$INSTALLED_SO"
24	echo "[prepare_env] replaced common_ops.abi3.so ($(stat -c%s "${SCRIPT_DIR}/common_ops.abi3.so") bytes)"
25	
26	# 5. Set inference parameters
27	#    Override SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS entirely (platform default has --attention-backend flashinfer,
28	#    we need minicpm_flashinfer; also add Medusa speculative decoding flags).
29	MEDUSA_WEIGHTS="${SCRIPT_DIR}/data/medusa_best.pt"
30	export SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS="--disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer --chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse --quantization modelopt_fp4 --max-running-requests 64 --mem-fraction-static 0.80 --speculative-algorithm MEDUSA --speculative-num-steps 1 --speculative-draft-model-path ${MEDUSA_WEIGHTS}"
31	export SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=36
32	export SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD=16
33	export CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=":4096:8"
34	
35	echo "[prepare_env] SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS=${SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS}"
36	echo "[prepare_env] SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=${SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD}"
37	echo "[prepare_env] done $(date '+%F %T')"
38

=== 1. demo-sala README ===
# MiniCPM-SALA 提交 Demo

本目录是一个最小可运行的提交示例，演示如何按照平台要求组织 `prepare_env.sh` + `prepare_model.sh` 提交包。

## 目录结构

```
.
├── prepare_env.sh          # 必须 — 环境构建脚本
├── prepare_model.sh        # 可选 — 模型预处理入口
├── preprocess_model.py     # prepare_model.sh 调用的 Python 脚本
└── sglang/python/          # 自定义 sglang 源码（editable install）
```

## 各文件说明

### `prepare_env.sh`（必须）

平台在基础环境启动后自动执行此脚本。本 demo 中做了两件事：

1. 用 `uv pip install --no-deps -e ./sglang/python` 将自定义 sglang 以 editable 模式安装，替换镜像内置版本
2. 通过 `export SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` 追加推理启动参数（示例中添加了 `--log-level info`）

```bash
uv pip install --no-deps -e ./sglang/python
export SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS="${SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS:-} --log-level info"
```

> **注意**：`prepare_env.sh` 会被 `source` 进入平台主脚本，因此 `export` 的环境变量可以直接生效。

### `prepare_model.sh`（可选）

平台在环境就绪后调用此脚本，接口固定为：

```bash
bash prepare_model.sh --input <原始模型路径> --output <处理后模型路径>
```

两个路径均由平台提供，选手无需关心容器内的具体挂载位置。本 demo 中仅做简单的模型文件复制，不做任何量化或转换。

实际参赛时，可以在 `preprocess_model.py` 中实现量化（GPTQ、AWQ 等）、剪枝、权重融合等预处理逻辑。

### `sglang/python/`

自定义的 sglang 源码目录。通过 editable install，平台会使用此目录下的代码替代镜像内置 sglang，选手可以在此修改推理引擎的实现。

## 扩展示例

| 场景 | 修改点 |
|---|---|
| 安装额外 pip 包 | `prepare_env.sh` 中添加 `uv pip install xxx` |
| 自定义推理参数 | `prepare_env.sh` 中修改 `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` |
| GPTQ 量化 | `preprocess_model.py` 中实现 GPTQ 打包，`prepare_env.sh` 中追加 `--quantization gptq` |
| 模型剪枝/蒸馏 | `preprocess_model.py` 中实现，输出到 `--output` 目录 |

=== 2. toolkit README ===
<div align="center">

# SOAR-Toolkit

<a href="README.md">中文</a> | <a href="README_EN.md">English</a>

</div>

---

本次比赛围绕 OpenBMB MiniCPM-SALA 模型的推理性能优化展开。参赛者需在指定的硬件环境上基于官方提供的 MiniCPM-SALA 模型进行优化，不得提交或替换任何基座模型。为了方便各位参赛选手进行自测，我们提供了以下评测工具，帮助大家进行检验和合理规划。主要包括：

# 基础镜像

## 镜像下载

我们提供了可运行模型并进行推理的基础镜像（内置 SGLang 及必要依赖），用于本地开发、调试与评测自测。

```bash
# 国内下载（阿里云ACR）
docker pull modelbest-registry.cn-beijing.cr.aliyuncs.com/public/soar-toolkit:latest

# 海外下载（Github）
docker pull ghcr.io/openbmb/soar-toolkit:latest
```

## MiniCPM-SALA 模型下载

### 方式一：通过 Hugging Face 下载
在下载前，请先通过如下命令安装 Hugging Face 官方 CLI 工具。
```bash
pip install huggingface_hub
```
下载完整模型库到指定路径文件夹./models
```bash
huggingface-cli download OpenBMB/MiniCPM-SALA --local-dir ./models
```

### 方式二：通过 ModelScope 下载
在下载前，请先通过如下命令安装 ModelScope。
```bash
pip install modelscope
```
下载完整模型库到指定路径文件夹./models
```bash
modelscope download --model OpenBMB/MiniCPM-SALA --local_dir ./models
```

## 容器内挂载地址
模型：`/models/MiniCPM-SALA`

## 容器启动脚本参考

### docker run 常用参数

| 参数 | 作用 | 示例 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `--gpus` | 选择可见 GPU | `--gpus '"device=0"'` |
| `-v <host>:<container>:ro` | 挂载目录/文件到容器（只读） | `-v /path/to/MiniCPM-SALA:/models/MiniCPM-SALA:ro` |
| `-p <host_port>:<container_port>` | 端口映射 | `-p 30000:30000` |
| `-e KEY=VALUE` | 传入环境变量（用于改启动参数） | `-e SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS='...'` |
| `--name <name>` | 容器命名（便于 `docker logs`） | `--name minicpm_sglang` |
| `-d` | 后台运行 | `-d` |
| `--rm` | 容器退出后自动删除 | `--rm` |

### 环境变量

| 环境变量 | 默认值 | 含义 / 对应 `sglang.launch_server` | 示例 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `MODEL_PATH` | `/models/MiniCPM-SALA` | `--model-path` | `-e MODEL_PATH=/models/MiniCPM-SALA` |
| `HOST` | `0.0.0.0` | `--host` | `-e HOST=0.0.0.0` |
| `PORT` | `30000` | `--port` | `-e PORT=30000` |
| `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` | `--disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer --chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse` | 若设置则覆盖默认参数，建议单引号包裹 | `-e SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS='--disable-radix-cache ...'` |

```bash
# 参考docker启动命令
docker run -d \
  --name soar-sglang-server \
  --gpus 'device=0' \
  -p 30000:30000 \
  -e SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS=[optional/可自定义部署参数，未设定时使用模型默认参数]'--xxxxx' \
  -v ~/models/MiniCPM-SALA:/models/MiniCPM-SALA:ro \
  modelbest-registry.cn-beijing.cr.aliyuncs.com/public/soar-toolkit:latest 
```
> 本地与线上评测环境可能存在差异，最终成绩以官方评测环境为准。

### 注意事项
- SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS 里请用连字符参数名：例如 `--dense-as-sparse`，不要写 `--dense_as_sparse`（镜像不做下划线自动转换）。

# 评测环境
为便于参赛选手有针对性地开展优化工作，现对评测环境说明如下：
- 参赛选手提交的全部文件总大小不得超过 2GB。
- 单次评测任务的资源上限为：20 CPU、128 GiB 内存。
- 单次评测任务的最长执行时间为 5 小时（不包含排队等待时间）。
- 硬件环境：本次比赛统一采用 NVIDIA 高端 RTX PRO 单卡 GPU 进行评测。

# 模型正确性评测
为了验证选手们对推理代码的优化不会影响模型在正确性上的表现，我们通过测试模型在特定数据集上的得分来进行评估。这里我们公开评测正确性所用的数据集`perf_public_set.jsonl`以及用于评测正确性的脚本`eval_model.py`，选手们也可以通过该数据集进行自查。

## perf_public_set.jsonl

> AGENT

明白了。根据官方文档：

1. `prepare_env.sh` 被 **source** 进平台主脚本，`export` 的环境变量直接生效
2. 平台默认 `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS`：`--disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer --chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse`
3. 选手设置的 `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` **会覆盖**默认值

现在看我们的设置：

```bash
MEDUSA_WEIGHTS="${SCRIPT_DIR}/data/medusa_best.pt"
export SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS="... --speculative-draft-model-path ${MEDUSA_WEIGHTS}"
```

**问题**：`${MEDUSA_WEIGHTS}` 在 export 时展开，但 `${SCRIPT_DIR}` 依赖 `${BASH_SOURCE[0]}`。当被 source 时，`BASH_SOURCE[0]` 是 prepare_env.sh 的路径，`SCRIPT_DIR` 应该正确解析为提交包目录。

**平台流程**（根据官方文档）：
1. 解压 tarball
2. `source prepare_env.sh` → 安装自定义 sglang、设置 `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS`
3. `bash prepare_model.sh --input ... --output ...` → 量化
4. 平台启动 sglang 服务器，**使用 `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` 环境变量**
5. 平台跑评测

**确认**：
- `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` **会被平台读取**（官方文档明确说 source 后 export 的变量直接生效）
- 路径 `${SCRIPT_DIR}/data/medusa_best.pt` 在 source 时正确展开为绝对路径
- 自定义 sglang 通过 editable install 覆盖平台内置版本，所以 `MEDUSA` 选项有效

# Claude Code Prompt Patcher - Gist Summary

## What This Is

This GitHub Gist contains a bash script (`patch-claude-code.sh`) that modifies Anthropic's Claude Code CLI to rebalance its system prompts. The creator argues that Claude Code has "15-20 separate places" instructing brevity versus only "3-4 instructions for thoroughness"—a problematic 5:1 ratio.

## Main Purpose

The script patches the npm-installed `@anthropic-ai/claude-code` package to shift the model from "minimal solutions" toward more thorough, production-quality work. Key changes include:

- Replacing "simplest approach first" with "correctly and completely solves the problem"
- Allowing error handling at system boundaries
- Permitting fixes to adjacent broken code when relevant
- Removing the "three lines better than abstraction" constraint

## Key Features

**11 prompt patches** target instructions the creator identifies as causing corner-cutting. The A/B test compared a ported physics engine (box2d) where unpatched Claude produced 1,419 lines with brute-force algorithms, while patched Claude generated 1,885 lines with dynamic AABB trees and soft contact constraints.

**Installation modes**: standard apply, `--watch` (auto-patch after updates), `--restore`, `--check`, `--dry-run`.

## Current Status

As of v2.1.100, Anthropic removed the three most aggressive brevity instructions upstream, reducing necessary patches from 11 to 8.
```

> AGENT

这是一个 **Claude Code 系统提示词补丁脚本** (`patch-claude-code.sh`)。

**问题**：作者认为 Claude Code 的系统提示里有 15-20 处强调"简洁"，但只有 3-4 处强调"完整"，5:1 的失衡导致模型倾向于产出最小可行方案而非生产级代码。

**补丁内容**（8-11 处修改）：
- "simplest approach first" → "correctly and completely solves the problem"
- 允许在系统边界添加错误处理
- 允许修复相邻的问题代码
- 移除"三行代码优于抽象"的约束

**A/B 测试**：移植 box2d 物理引擎
- 未打补丁：1,419 行，暴力算法
- 打补丁后：1,885 行，动态 AABB 树 + 软接触约束

**安装方式**：`--apply`、`--watch`（自动重打补丁）、`--restore`、`--check`、`--dry-run`

**当前状态**：Anthropic 在 v2.1.100 已移除了 3 条最激进的简洁指令，补丁从 11 处减少到 8 处。

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# patch-claude-code.sh — Rebalance Claude Code prompts to fix corner-cutting behavior
#
# What this does:
#   Patches the npm-installed @anthropic-ai/claude-code cli.js to rebalance
#   system prompt instructions that cause the model to cut corners, simplify
#   excessively, and defer complicated work.
#
# Requirements:
#   - Node.js >= 18
#   - npm
#
# Usage:
#   ./patch-claude-code.sh              # install (if needed), patch, repoint claude binary
#   ./patch-claude-code.sh --restore    # restore original cli.js and repoint to bun binary
#   ./patch-claude-code.sh --dry-run    # show what would be patched without modifying anything
#   ./patch-claude-code.sh --check      # check if patches are already applied
#   ./patch-claude-code.sh --watch      # install watcher to re-patch after auto-updates
#   ./patch-claude-code.sh --unwatch    # remove the watcher

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
SCRIPT_PATH="$SCRIPT_DIR/$(basename "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")"
OS="$(uname -s)"

# Watcher identifiers
PLIST_LABEL="com.user.claude-code-patcher"
PLIST_PATH="$HOME/Library/LaunchAgents/$PLIST_LABEL.plist"
SYSTEMD_UNIT="claude-code-patcher.path"
SYSTEMD_SERVICE="claude-code-patcher.service"
SYSTEMD_DIR="$HOME/.config/systemd/user"

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Resolve paths
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

find_claude_bin() {
  local candidates=(
    "$(command -v claude 2>/dev/null || true)"
    "$HOME/.local/bin/claude"
    "$HOME/.claude/bin/claude"
    "/usr/local/bin/claude"
  )
  for c in "${candidates[@]}"; do
    if [[ -n "$c" && -e "$c" ]]; then
      echo "$c"
      return 0
    fi
  done
  return 1
}

find_bun_binary() {
  local versions_dir="$HOME/.local/share/claude/versions"
  if [[ -d "$versions_dir" ]]; then
    local latest
    latest=$(ls -1 "$versions_dir" 2>/dev/null | sort -V | tail -1)
    if [[ -n "$latest" ]]; then
      echo "$versions_dir/$latest"
      return 0
    fi
  fi
  return 1
}

ensure_npm_package() {
  local npm_user_4813494d
  npm_user_4813494d="$(npm user_4813494d -g 2>/dev/null)" || {
    echo "ERROR: npm not found or npm user_4813494d -g failed" >&2
    exit 1
  }

  local cli_js="$npm_user_4813494d/@anthropic-ai/claude-code/cli.js"

  if [[ ! -f "$cli_js" ]]; then
    echo "Claude Code npm package not found. Installing..." >&2
    npm install -g @anthropic-ai/claude-code || {
      echo "ERROR: Failed to install @anthropic-ai/claude-code" >&2
      exit 1
    }
  fi

  echo "$cli_js"
}

get_version() {
  local cli_js="$1"
  local pkg_json
  pkg_json="$(dirname "$cli_js")/package.json"
  if [[ -f "$pkg_json" ]]; then
    node -e "console.log(require('$pkg_json').version)" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown"
  else
    node -e "
      const src = require('fs').readFileSync('$cli_js', 'utf8');
      const m = src.match(/Version: ([\\d.]+)/);
      console.log(m ? m[1] : 'unknown');
    " 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown"
  fi
}

217	);

233	  "Match the scope of your actions to what was actually requested.",
234	  "Match the scope of your actions to what was actually requested, but do address closely related issues you discover during the work when fixing them is clearly the right thing to do."
235	);
236	
237	// ===========================================================================
238	// Results
239	// ===========================================================================
240	if (checkOnly) {

242	  process.exit(alreadyApplied === 11 ? 0 : 1);
243	}

247	}

249	if (dryRun) console.log("(dry run — no files modified)");

## 补丁前后对比

| # | 名称 | 原文 | 补丁后 |
|---|------|------|--------|
| 1 | Output efficiency | "Try the **simplest approach first**" | "Choose the approach that **correctly and completely solves** the problem" |
| 2 | Brevity paragraph | "Keep text output brief" | 增加 "Note: these guidelines apply to messages, **NOT to thoroughness of code**" |
| 3 | One sentence | "If you can say it in one sentence, don't use three" | "Prefer short sentences... **does not apply to code or thoroughness**" |
| 4 | Anti-gold-plating | "Don't make improvements beyond what was asked" | "If adjacent code is **broken or fragile**, fix it as part of the task" |
| 5 | Error handling | "**Don't add** error handling for scenarios that can't happen" | "**Add** error handling at real boundaries (I/O, network, user input)" |
| 6 | Three lines rule | "Three similar lines is **better than** abstraction" | "Use **judgment** about when to extract shared logic" |
| 7 | Subagent gold-plate | "Complete fully—don't gold-plate" | "Do work a **careful senior developer** would do, including edge cases" |
| 8 | Explore agent | "You are meant to be a **fast** agent" | "Be **thorough**... do not sacrifice completeness for speed" |
| 9 | Tone | "Responses should be **short and concise**" | "Responses should be **clear and appropriately detailed**" |
| 10 | Code snippets | "Include snippets **only** when exact text is load-bearing" | "Include snippets when they provide **useful context**" |
| 11 | Scope matching | "Match the scope of your actions to what was requested" | "...but do address **closely related issues** you discover" |

**状态**：补丁 1-3 已被 Anthropic 上游移除，补丁 4-11（共 8 处）已应用。

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -euo pipefail
3	# probe prepare_model.sh — quantize, self-eval with Medusa, email results, exit 1

5	SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
6	
7	echo "[probe] start $(date '+%F %T')"
8	echo "[probe] args: $*"

10	# Parse --input / --output

12	OUTPUT=""

20

38	env = collect_env_info()

40	" 2>&1 || echo "[probe] pre-quant email failed (non-fatal)"
41	
42	echo "[probe] starting quantization (GPTQ+FourOverSix, 48K, loguniform128)"
43	python3 "${SCRIPT_DIR}/preprocess_model.py" --input "$INPUT" --output "$OUTPUT" 2>&1 | tee "${SCRIPT_DIR}/quant_log.txt"
44	echo "[probe] quantization done $(date '+%F %T')"
45	
46	# ── Self-eval + email ──
47	echo "[probe] starting probe_eval..."
48	python3 "${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_eval.py" "$OUTPUT" 2>&1 || true
49	
50	echo "[probe] probe complete $(date '+%F %T'), exiting 1 to skip official eval"
51	exit 1

probe_eval.py — Platform probe: quantize, self-eval, email results, exit 1.

attachment.

from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
import json
import os
import re
import smtplib
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from email import encoders
from email.mime.base import MIMEBase
from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart
from email.mime.text import MIMEText
from pathlib import Path

import requests

# ── Config ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
SMTP_HOST = "smtp.qq.com"

SMTP_PASS = "srllfldhbmxidaag"

SERVER_PORT = 30001
API_BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{SERVER_PORT}"

# ── Email ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def send_email(subject: str, body: str, attachments: dict[str, bytes] | None = None):
    """Send email with optional attachments. attachments = {filename: bytes_content}."""
    msg = MIMEMultipart()
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg["From"] = SMTP_USER
    msg["To"] = EMAIL_TO
    msg.attach(MIMEText(body, "plain", "utf-8"))

    for fname, data in (attachments or {}).items():
        part = MIMEBase("application", "octet-stream")
        part.set_payload(data)
        encoders.encode_base64(part)
        part.add_header("Content-Disposition", "attachment", filename=fname)
        msg.attach(part)

    try:
        with smtplib.SMTP_SSL(SMTP_HOST, SMTP_PORT, timeout=30) as s:
            s.login(SMTP_USER, SMTP_PASS)
            s.send_message(msg)
        print(f"[probe] email sent: {subject}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[probe] email FAILED: {e}", file=sys.stderr)


# ── Env info ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    lines = []

> AGENT

**找到根因**：`set -u` + `$2` unbound variable！

如果参数格式有任何问题，脚本会**静默退出**，没有任何输出。

修复方案：改成和 demo-sala 一样用 `"$@"` 直接传参，或者把 `set -u` 改掉。

> AGENT

**修复完成**：

根因：`set -euo pipefail` 的 `-u` 选项 + 参数解析时 `$2` 可能不存在 → **静默退出**，没有任何输出。

修复：
1. `set -euo pipefail` → `set -eo pipefail`（去掉 `-u`）
2. `$2` → `${2:-}`（默认空字符串）
3. `shift 2` → `shift; shift || true`（不会因为参数不足而退出）

## probe-sala 当前流程

**平台执行**：
```
source prepare_env.sh → bash prepare_model.sh --input <BF16> --output <dst>
```

### 1. prepare_env.sh（平台 source）
- 安装自定义 sglang（editable）
- 安装 nvidia-modelopt + llmcompressor
- patch FourOverSix 到 llmcompressor
- 替换 common_ops.abi3.so（Marlin 修复）
- 设置环境变量：`SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS`、`SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=36`、`SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD=16`

### 2. prepare_model.sh
```
① 发邮件 (probe_email.py) → 失败则 exit 1
② 量化 (preprocess_model.py) → GPTQ+FourOverSix, loguniform128, 48K
③ probe_eval.py:
   - 发邮件 1/3 (quant_log + env)
   - 启 sglang 服务器 (端口 30001, Medusa K=1)
   - 等 240 秒
   - 150 样本并发评测 (concurrency=32)
   - 发邮件 3/3 (accuracy + predictions.gz + summary.json)
   - kill 服务器
④ exit 1 → 跳过平台官方评测
```

### 邮件时间线
| 邮件 | 时机 | 内容 |
|------|------|------|
| **probe_email** | 量化前 | hostname + python + args |
| **1/3** | 量化后 | quant_log + 环境信息 |
| **3/3** | eval完成 | accuracy + predictions.jsonl.gz |

> AGENT

合理。tmp 只是**中间过程**：

```
1. calib_dir = /tmp/calib_xxx/     ← 校准数据（临时）
2. llmc_dir = /tmp/gptq_llmc_xxx/  ← llmcompressor 中间输出（临时）
3. phase2_convert → dst (--output) ← 最终模型写到平台指定的 --output
4. 清理 calib_dir 和 llmc_dir
```

最终量化模型会在 `--output` 路径，不在 tmp。

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# prepare_env.sh — sourced by platform after base image startup
3	# Do NOT set -euo pipefail here (sourced script should not exit the parent shell)
4	
5	echo "[prepare_env] start $(date '+%F %T')"
6	
7	SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
8	
9	# 1. Install our custom SGLang (replaces image built-in version)
10	uv pip install --no-deps -e "${SCRIPT_DIR}/sglang/python"
11	
12	# 2. Install nvidia-modelopt (inference) + llmcompressor (GPTQ quantization)
13	uv pip install "nvidia-modelopt==0.42.0" "llmcompressor==[REDACTED]"
14	
15	# 3. Patch llmcompressor: FourOverSix adaptive scale=4/6 selection (arXiv:2512.02010)
16	GPTQ_TARGET=[REDACTED]
17	cp "${SCRIPT_DIR}/patches/gptq_quantize_fouroversix.py" "$GPTQ_TARGET"
18	echo "[prepare_env] patched gptq_quantize.py with FourOverSix"
19	
20	# 4. Replace sgl-kernel .so with pre-built version (Marlin FP4 scale fix)
21	INSTALLED_SO="/opt/SGLang-MiniCPM-SALA/sglang_minicpm_sala_env/lib/python3.10/site-packages/sgl_kernel/sm100/common_ops.abi3.so"
22	cp "$INSTALLED_SO" "${INSTALLED_SO}.bak"
23	cp "${SCRIPT_DIR}/common_ops.abi3.so" "$INSTALLED_SO"
24	echo "[prepare_env] replaced common_ops.abi3.so ($(stat -c%s "${SCRIPT_DIR}/common_ops.abi3.so") bytes)"
25	
26	# 5. Set inference parameters
27	#    Override SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS entirely (platform default has --attention-backend flashinfer,
28	#    we need minicpm_flashinfer; also add Medusa speculative decoding flags).
29	MEDUSA_WEIGHTS="${SCRIPT_DIR}/data/medusa_best.pt"
30	export SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS="--disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer --chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse --quantization modelopt_fp4 --max-running-requests 64 --mem-fraction-static 0.80 --speculative-algorithm MEDUSA --speculative-num-steps 1 --speculative-draft-model-path ${MEDUSA_WEIGHTS}"
31	export SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=36
32	export SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD=16
33	export CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=":4096:8"
34	
35	echo "[prepare_env] SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS=${SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS}"
36	echo "[prepare_env] SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=${SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD}"
37	echo "[prepare_env] done $(date '+%F %T')"
38

1	import ctypes
2	import glob
3	import importlib.util

7	from pathlib import Path
8	from typing import List
9	
10	import torch
11	
12	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
13	
14	
15	def _get_compute_capability():
16	    """Get the compute capability of the current GPU."""
17	    if not torch.cuda.is_available():
18	        return None
19	
20	    # Get the current device
21	    device = torch.cuda.current_device()
22	    properties = torch.cuda.get_device_properties(device)
23	
24	    # Return as integer (major * 10 + minor)
25	    return properties.major * 10 + properties.minor
26	
27	
28	def _filter_compiled_extensions(file_list):
29	    """Filter and prioritize compiled extensions over Python source files."""
30	    compiled_extensions = [".so", ".pyd", ".dll"]  # Common compiled extension suffixes
31	    compiled_files = []
32	    other_files = []
33	
34	    for file_path in file_list:
35	        path = Path(file_path)
36	        # Check if it's a compiled extension (including complex names like .abi3.so, .cpython-312.so)
37	        if any(
38	            str(path).endswith(ext) or ext in str(path) for ext in compiled_extensions
39	        ):
40	            compiled_files.append(file_path)
41	        else:
42	            other_files.append(file_path)
43	
44	    # Return compiled files first, then others
45	    return compiled_files + other_files
46	
47	
48	def _load_architecture_specific_ops():
49	    """Load the appropriate common_ops library based on GPU architecture."""
50	    compute_capability = _get_compute_capability()
51	    logger.debug(
52	        f"[sgl_kernel] GPU Detection: compute_capability = {compute_capability}"
53	    )
54	
55	    # Get the directory where sgl_kernel is installed
56	    sgl_kernel_dir = Path(__file__).parent
57	    logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] sgl_kernel directory: {sgl_kernel_dir}")
58	
59	    # Determine which version to load based on GPU architecture
60	    if compute_capability == 90:
61	        ops_subdir = "sm90"
62	        variant_name = "SM90 (Hopper/H100 with fast math optimization)"
63	    elif compute_capability is not None:
64	        ops_subdir = "sm100"
65	        variant_name = f"SM{compute_capability} (precise math for compatibility)"
66	    else:
67	        ops_subdir = "sm100"
68	        variant_name = "CPU/No GPU detected (using precise math)"
69	
70	    # Look for the compiled module with any valid extension
71	
72	    ops_pattern = str(sgl_kernel_dir / ops_subdir / "common_ops.*")
73	    raw_matching_files = glob.glob(ops_pattern)
74	    matching_files = _filter_compiled_extensions(raw_matching_files)
75	
76	    logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Attempting to load {variant_name}")
77	    logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Looking for library matching pattern: {ops_pattern}")
78	    logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Found files: {raw_matching_files}")
79	    logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Prioritized files: {matching_files}")
80	
81	    previous_import_errors: List[Exception] = []
82	
83	    # Try to load from the architecture-specific directory
84	    if matching_files:
85	        ops_path = Path(matching_files[0])  # Use the first prioritized file
86	        logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Found architecture-specific library: {ops_path}")
87	        try:
88	            # Load the module from specific path using importlib
89	            spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("common_ops", str(ops_path))
90	            if spec is None:
91	                raise ImportError(f"Could not create module spec for {ops_path}")
92	
93	            common_ops = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
94	            if spec.loader is None:
95	                raise ImportError(f"Module spec has no loader for {ops_path}")
96	
97	            logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Loading module from {ops_path}...")
98	            spec.loader.exec_module(common_ops)
99	            logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] ✓ Successfully loaded {variant_name}")
100	            logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] ✓ Module file: {common_ops.__file__}")
101	            return common_ops
102	
103	        except Exception as e:
104	            previous_import_errors.append(e)
105	            logger.debug(
106	                f"[sgl_kernel] ✗ Failed to load from {ops_path}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
107	            )
108	            # Continue to fallback
109	    else:
110	        logger.debug(
111	            f"[sgl_kernel] ✗ Architecture-specific library not found matching pattern: {ops_pattern}"
112	        )
113	
114	    # Try alternative directory (in case installation structure differs)
115	    alt_pattern = str(sgl_kernel_dir / "common_ops.*")
116	    raw_alt_files = glob.glob(alt_pattern)
117	    alt_matching_files = _filter_compiled_extensions(raw_alt_files)
118	    logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Attempting fallback: looking for pattern {alt_pattern}")
119	    logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Found fallback files: {raw_alt_files}")
120	    logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Prioritized fallback files: {alt_matching_files}")
121	
122	    if alt_matching_files:
123	        alt_path = Path(alt_matching_files[0])  # Use the first prioritized file
124	        logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Found fallback library: {alt_path}")
125	        try:
126	            spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("common_ops", str(alt_path))
127	            if spec is None:
128	                raise ImportError(f"Could not create module spec for {alt_path}")
129	
130	            common_ops = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
131	            if spec.loader is None:
132	                raise ImportError(f"Module spec has no loader for {alt_path}")
133	
134	            logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] Loading fallback module from {alt_path}...")
135	            spec.loader.exec_module(common_ops)
136	            logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] ✓ Successfully loaded fallback library")
137	            logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] ✓ Module file: {common_ops.__file__}")
138	            return common_ops
139	
140	        except Exception as e:
141	            previous_import_errors.append(e)
142	            logger.debug(
143	                f"[sgl_kernel] ✗ Failed to load fallback from {alt_path}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
144	            )
145	    else:
146	        logger.debug(
147	            f"[sgl_kernel] ✗ Fallback library not found matching pattern: {alt_pattern}"
148	        )
149	
150	    # Final attempt: try standard Python import (for backward compatibility)
151	    logger.debug(
152	        f"[sgl_kernel] Final attempt: trying standard Python import 'common_ops'"
153	    )
154	    try:
155	        import common_ops
156	
157	        logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] ✓ Successfully imported via standard Python import")
158	        logger.debug(f"[sgl_kernel] ✓ Module file: {common_ops.__file__}")

163

191	# copy & modify from torch/utils/cpp_extension.py
192	def _find_cuda_home():
193	    """Find the CUDA install path."""
194	    # Guess #1
195	    cuda_home = os.environ.get("CUDA_HOME") or os.environ.get("CUDA_PATH")
196	    if cuda_home is None:
197	        # Guess #2
198	        nvcc_path = shutil.which("nvcc")
199	        if nvcc_path is not None:
200	            cuda_home = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(nvcc_path))
201	        else:
202	            # Guess #3
203	            cuda_home = "/usr/local/cuda"
204	    return cuda_home
205	
206	
207	def _preload_cuda_library():
208	    """Preload the CUDA runtime library to help avoid 'libcudart.so.12 not found' issues."""
209	    cuda_home = Path(_find_cuda_home())
210

> AGENT

找到了！**chunk_cache.py** 关键差异：

demo-sala（正确）:
```python
k1_indices = k1_indices[k1_indices > 0]
if k1_indices.numel() > 0:
    self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.free(k1_indices)
```

probe-sala（缺失）:
```python
self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.free(k1_indices)  # 没有过滤零索引！
```

这会在speculative decoding时free未分配的slots，可能导致内存corruption。

要同步这些文件到probe-sala吗？

<div align="center">

# SOAR-Toolkit

<a href="README.md">中文</a> | <a href="README_EN.md">English</a>

</div>

---

本次比赛围绕 OpenBMB MiniCPM-SALA 模型的推理性能优化展开。参赛者需在指定的硬件环境上基于官方提供的 MiniCPM-SALA 模型进行优化，不得提交或替换任何基座模型。为了方便各位参赛选手进行自测，我们提供了以下评测工具，帮助大家进行检验和合理规划。主要包括：

# 基础镜像

## 镜像下载

我们提供了可运行模型并进行推理的基础镜像（内置 SGLang 及必要依赖），用于本地开发、调试与评测自测。

```bash
# 国内下载（阿里云ACR）
docker pull modelbest-registry.cn-beijing.cr.aliyuncs.com/public/soar-toolkit:latest

# 海外下载（Github）
docker pull ghcr.io/openbmb/soar-toolkit:latest
```

## MiniCPM-SALA 模型下载

### 方式一：通过 Hugging Face 下载
在下载前，请先通过如下命令安装 Hugging Face 官方 CLI 工具。
```bash
pip install huggingface_hub
```
下载完整模型库到指定路径文件夹./models
```bash
huggingface-cli download OpenBMB/MiniCPM-SALA --local-dir ./models
```

### 方式二：通过 ModelScope 下载
在下载前，请先通过如下命令安装 ModelScope。
```bash
pip install modelscope
```
下载完整模型库到指定路径文件夹./models
```bash
modelscope download --model OpenBMB/MiniCPM-SALA --local_dir ./models
```

## 容器内挂载地址
模型：`/models/MiniCPM-SALA`

## 容器启动脚本参考

### docker run 常用参数

| 参数 | 作用 | 示例 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `--gpus` | 选择可见 GPU | `--gpus '"device=0"'` |
| `-v <host>:<container>:ro` | 挂载目录/文件到容器（只读） | `-v /path/to/MiniCPM-SALA:/models/MiniCPM-SALA:ro` |
| `-p <host_port>:<container_port>` | 端口映射 | `-p 30000:30000` |
| `-e KEY=VALUE` | 传入环境变量（用于改启动参数） | `-e SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS='...'` |
| `--name <name>` | 容器命名（便于 `docker logs`） | `--name minicpm_sglang` |
| `-d` | 后台运行 | `-d` |
| `--rm` | 容器退出后自动删除 | `--rm` |

### 环境变量

| 环境变量 | 默认值 | 含义 / 对应 `sglang.launch_server` | 示例 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `MODEL_PATH` | `/models/MiniCPM-SALA` | `--model-path` | `-e MODEL_PATH=/models/MiniCPM-SALA` |
| `HOST` | `0.0.0.0` | `--host` | `-e HOST=0.0.0.0` |
| `PORT` | `30000` | `--port` | `-e PORT=30000` |
| `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` | `--disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer --chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse` | 若设置则覆盖默认参数，建议单引号包裹 | `-e SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS='--disable-radix-cache ...'` |

```bash
# 参考docker启动命令
docker run -d \
  --name soar-sglang-server \
  --gpus 'device=0' \
  -p 30000:30000 \
  -e SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS=[optional/可自定义部署参数，未设定时使用模型默认参数]'--xxxxx' \
  -v ~/models/MiniCPM-SALA:/models/MiniCPM-SALA:ro \
  modelbest-registry.cn-beijing.cr.aliyuncs.com/public/soar-toolkit:latest 
```
> 本地与线上评测环境可能存在差异，最终成绩以官方评测环境为准。

### 注意事项
- SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS 里请用连字符参数名：例如 `--dense-as-sparse`，不要写 `--dense_as_sparse`（镜像不做下划线自动转换）。

# 评测环境
为便于参赛选手有针对性地开展优化工作，现对评测环境说明如下：
- 参赛选手提交的全部文件总大小不得超过 2GB。
- 单次评测任务的资源上限为：20 CPU、128 GiB 内存。
- 单次评测任务的最长执行时间为 5 小时（不包含排队等待时间）。
- 硬件环境：本次比赛统一采用 NVIDIA 高端 RTX PRO 单卡 GPU 进行评测。

# 模型正确性评测
为了验证选手们对推理代码的优化不会影响模型在正确性上的表现，我们通过测试模型在特定数据集上的得分来进行评估。这里我们公开评测正确性所用的数据集`perf_public_set.jsonl`以及用于评测正确性的脚本`eval_model.py`，选手们也可以通过该数据集进行自查。

## perf_public_set.jsonl
```

# MiniCPM-SALA 提交 Demo

本目录是一个最小可运行的提交示例，演示如何按照平台要求组织 `prepare_env.sh` + `prepare_model.sh` 提交包。

## 目录结构

```
.
├── prepare_env.sh          # 必须 — 环境构建脚本
├── prepare_model.sh        # 可选 — 模型预处理入口
├── preprocess_model.py     # prepare_model.sh 调用的 Python 脚本
└── sglang/python/          # 自定义 sglang 源码（editable install）
```

## 各文件说明

### `prepare_env.sh`（必须）

平台在基础环境启动后自动执行此脚本。本 demo 中做了两件事：

1. 用 `uv pip install --no-deps -e ./sglang/python` 将自定义 sglang 以 editable 模式安装，替换镜像内置版本
2. 通过 `export SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` 追加推理启动参数（示例中添加了 `--log-level info`）

```bash
uv pip install --no-deps -e ./sglang/python
export SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS="${SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS:-} --log-level info"
```

> **注意**：`prepare_env.sh` 会被 `source` 进入平台主脚本，因此 `export` 的环境变量可以直接生效。

### `prepare_model.sh`（可选）

平台在环境就绪后调用此脚本，接口固定为：

```bash
bash prepare_model.sh --input <原始模型路径> --output <处理后模型路径>
```

两个路径均由平台提供，选手无需关心容器内的具体挂载位置。本 demo 中仅做简单的模型文件复制，不做任何量化或转换。

实际参赛时，可以在 `preprocess_model.py` 中实现量化（GPTQ、AWQ 等）、剪枝、权重融合等预处理逻辑。

### `sglang/python/`

自定义的 sglang 源码目录。通过 editable install，平台会使用此目录下的代码替代镜像内置 sglang，选手可以在此修改推理引擎的实现。

## 扩展示例

| 场景 | 修改点 |
|---|---|
| 安装额外 pip 包 | `prepare_env.sh` 中添加 `uv pip install xxx` |
| 自定义推理参数 | `prepare_env.sh` 中修改 `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS` |
| GPTQ 量化 | `preprocess_model.py` 中实现 GPTQ 打包，`prepare_env.sh` 中追加 `--quantization gptq` |
| 模型剪枝/蒸馏 | `preprocess_model.py` 中实现，输出到 `--output` 目录 |
```

> AGENT

SM: (12, 0) → 用sm100目录，路径正确。

**关键问题**：如果平台在source prepare_env.sh之前已经import过sgl_kernel（比如做环境检查），.so就已经加载到内存了。之后替换物理文件不会重新加载。

让我验证这个假设——检查平台是否可能预先import：

  "description": "验证Python模块加载缓存问题"
}

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# prepare_env.sh — sourced by platform after base image startup
# Do NOT set -euo pipefail here (sourced script should not exit the parent shell)

echo "[prepare_env] start $(date '+%F %T')"

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"

# 1. Install our custom SGLang (replaces image built-in version)
uv pip install --no-deps -e "${SCRIPT_DIR}/sglang/python"

# 2. Install nvidia-modelopt (required for NVFP4 quantization in prepare_model.sh
#    and for loading modelopt_fp4 checkpoints at inference time)
uv pip install "nvidia-modelopt==0.42.0" "accelerate==1.13.0"

# 3. Patch sgl-kernel Marlin FP4 scale handling and rebuild
SGL_KERNEL_DIR="/opt/SGLang-MiniCPM-SALA/packages/sglang-minicpm/sgl-kernel"
echo "[prepare_env] patching sgl-kernel marlin_template.h"
patch -N -p1 -d "$SGL_KERNEL_DIR" < "${SCRIPT_DIR}/patches/marlin_fp4_scale.patch" \
  || echo "[prepare_env] patch already applied, skipping"
apt-get install -y -qq libibverbs-dev 2>/dev/null || true
# Ensure no stale proxy blocks git during CMake FetchContent
unset http_proxy https_proxy HTTP_PROXY HTTPS_PROXY ALL_PROXY all_proxy
git config --global --unset http.proxy 2>/dev/null || true
git config --global --unset https.proxy 2>/dev/null || true
echo "[prepare_env] rebuilding sgl-kernel (MAX_JOBS=32)"
DEPS_DIR="${SCRIPT_DIR}/sgl-kernel-deps"
CMAKE_ARGS="\
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_REPO-CUTLASS=$DEPS_DIR/repo-cutlass \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_REPO-DEEPGEMM=$DEPS_DIR/repo-deepgemm \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_REPO-FMT=$DEPS_DIR/repo-fmt \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_REPO-TRITON=$DEPS_DIR/repo-triton \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_REPO-FLASHINFER=$DEPS_DIR/repo-flashinfer \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_REPO-FLASH-ATTENTION=$DEPS_DIR/repo-flash-attention \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_REPO-MSCCLPP=$DEPS_DIR/repo-mscclpp \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_REPO-FAST-HADAMARD-TRANSFORM=$DEPS_DIR/repo-fast-hadamard-transform \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_REPO-FLASHMLA=$DEPS_DIR/repo-flashmla \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_NANOBIND=$DEPS_DIR/nanobind \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_DLPACK=$DEPS_DIR/dlpack \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_SOURCE_DIR_JSON=$DEPS_DIR/json \
  -DFETCHCONTENT_FULLY_DISCONNECTED=ON \
  -DENABLE_BELOW_SM90=OFF"
export CMAKE_ARGS
# Strip all gencode except sm_120a to speed up compilation and avoid OOM
sed -i '/-gencode=arch=compute_8/d; /-gencode=arch=compute_90/d; /-gencode=arch=compute_100a/d; /-gencode=arch=compute_10[13]/d; /-gencode=arch=compute_11/d; /-gencode=arch=compute_121/d' \
  "$SGL_KERNEL_DIR/CMakeLists.txt"
# Build only common_ops_sm100 target (contains marlin kernel), then replace .so in-place
BUILD_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
echo "[prepare_env] cmake configure..."
TORCH_CMAKE="$(python3 -c 'import torch; print(torch.utils.cmake_prefix_path)')"
(cd "$SGL_KERNEL_DIR" && cmake -B "$BUILD_DIR" -G Ninja \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH="$TORCH_CMAKE" \
  -DPYTHON_EXECUTABLE="$(which python3)" \
  -DPython_EXECUTABLE="$(which python3)" \
  -DPython3_EXECUTABLE="$(which python3)" \
  $CMAKE_ARGS)
echo "[prepare_env] building common_ops_sm100_build (MAX_JOBS=32)..."
cmake --build "$BUILD_DIR" --target common_ops_sm100_build -j 2
# Replace the installed .so
INSTALLED_SO="/opt/SGLang-MiniCPM-SALA/sglang_minicpm_sala_env/lib/python3.10/site-packages/sgl_kernel/sm100/common_ops.abi3.so"
BUILT_SO=$(find "$BUILD_DIR" -name "common_ops*.so" | head -1)
if [ -n "$BUILT_SO" ]; then
  cp "$INSTALLED_SO" "${INSTALLED_SO}.bak"
  cp "$BUILT_SO" "$INSTALLED_SO"
  echo "[prepare_env] replaced $INSTALLED_SO"
else
  echo "[prepare_env] ERROR: built .so not found" >&2
fi

# 4. Append our inference parameters to SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS.
#    The platform default already sets:
#      --disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer
#      --chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse
#    We add quantization on top.
export SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS="${SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS:-} \
  --quantization modelopt_fp4"
echo "[prepare_env] SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS=${SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS}"
echo "[prepare_env] done $(date '+%F %T')"
```

```
# Technical Notes

## 1. SGLang Integration Fixes

All fixes verified with platform submission ori_accuracy=78.98% (2026-03-27).

### 1.1 NVFP4_AWQ Name Normalization

modelopt exports `quant_algo: "NVFP4_AWQ"`, SGLang only recognizes `"NVFP4"`. Fix in `modelopt_quant.py`:
```python
if quant_method.startswith("NVFP4"):
    quant_method = "NVFP4"
```

### 1.2 AWQ pre_quant_scale Support

AWQ smooths each input channel with `pre_quant_scale`; inference must multiply activations by this scale. Without it: garbled output.

Three changes in `modelopt_quant.py`:
- `from_config`: parse `has_pre_quant_scale` from nested/flat config
- `create_weights`: register Parameter when enabled
- `apply`: `x = x * layer.pre_quant_scale` before `fp4_quantize`

### 1.3 Unknown Tensor KeyError

Quantized checkpoints contain auxiliary tensors not in SGLang's model graph. Fix in `minicpm.py`:
```python
if name not in params_dict:
    continue
```

### 1.4 config.json Missing AWQ Metadata

modelopt export omits `pre_quant_scale: true` from config.json. `preprocess_model.py` patches it post-export along with `group_size` and `has_zero_point`.

### 1.5 max_position_embeddings / sparse_config Restoration

Calibration temporarily shrinks `max_position_embeddings` and disables `sparse_config`. Must restore before export, otherwise InfLLM-v2 sparse attention is disabled and 80% of long-context prompts regress.

### 1.6 CUDA Graph kv_indptr Corruption

flashinfer graph replay overwrites precomputed sparse kv_indptr buffer. Fix in `minicpm_backend.py`: save immutable backup at init, restore via `copy_` before each `begin_forward`.

## 2. NVFP4 Quantization Analysis

### 2.1 Current Strategy

- Algorithm: `awq_lite` (NVIDIA ModelOpt preset)
- Format: FP4 E2M1, block_size=16, FP8 E4M3 block scales, per-tensor global scale
- Coverage: 256 Linear layers (all gate/up/down/q/k/v/o/z_proj), lm_head excluded
- Calibration: 90 samples (30 mcq + 30 niah + 30 qa), fwe/cwe excluded

### 2.2 Layer Sensitivity (High → Low)

1. `lm_head` (excluded from quantization)
2. `down_proj` — MLP bottleneck, writes back to residual
3. `z_proj` / `o_gate` — sigmoid gating amplifies quantization error
4. `gate_proj` — MLP multiplicative gate
5. `q_proj` / `k_proj` — affects retrieval; qk_norm partially mitigates
6. `v_proj` / `o_proj` / `up_proj` — most tolerant

### 2.3 Parameter Distribution

| Module | Params | Count |
|--------|--------|-------|
| gate/up/down_proj | 2.15B each | 32 each |
| q_proj / o_proj | 537M each | 32 each |
| z_proj | 403M | 24 (Lightning Attention) |
| k_proj / v_proj | 34M each | 32 each |
| o_gate | 134M | 8 (standard Attention) |
| lm_head | 301M | 1 (not quantized) |

## 3. Decode Optimization Research

### 3.1 Baseline Analysis

M=1 decode is **bandwidth-bound** (arithmetic intensity ~2-4 FLOP/B, roofline crossover ~140 FLOP/B).

| Kernel | Weight Size | Bandwidth Utilization | tok/s |
|--------|------------|----------------------|-------|
| NVFP4 CUTLASS | 4.3 GB | **39%** | 80 |
| FP8 torch._scaled_mm | 7.6 GB | 71% | 82 |
| BF16 cuBLAS | 15.2 GB | 93% | 58 |

NVFP4 has smallest weights but worst bandwidth utilization. The CUTLASS kernel uses 128x128x128 tiles — 127/128 rows wasted at M=1.

### 3.2 Marlin W4A16 Breakthrough

Marlin (`gptq_marlin_gemm`) is purpose-built for weight-only quantization GEMV:

| Op | K | N | Marlin | NVFP4 CUTLASS | Speedup |
|----|---|---|--------|--------------|---------|
| qkv_proj | 4096 | 4608 | 8.3 us | 36.7 us | 4.4x |
| gate_up | 4096 | 32768 | 20.3 us | 90.2 us | 4.4x |
| down | 16384 | 4096 | 14.4 us | 159.4 us | 11.1x |
| **32-layer total** | | | **1.7 ms** | **10.3 ms** | **6.0x** |

Projected decode: **227 tok/s** vs 80 tok/s (2.8x).

### 3.3 Marlin vs CUTLASS by Batch Size

| M | Marlin | CUTLASS | Winner |
|---|--------|---------|--------|
| 1-32 | faster | — | Marlin (2.5-6x) |
| ~64 | — | — | Crossover point |
| 64+ | — | faster | CUTLASS (1.4-3.8x) |

### 3.4 E2E Marlin Results (Full Marlin Path)

Using `SGLANG_FORCE_NVFP4_MARLIN=1` (all inference through Marlin, no hybrid):

| Benchmark | Marlin | CUTLASS | Speedup |
|-----------|--------|---------|---------|
| mini_bench S1 | 270s | 423s | 1.57x |
| mini_bench S8 | 350s | 445s | 1.27x |
| mini_bench Smax | 469s | 520s | 1.11x |

S1 gains largest (decode-dominated); Smax gains smallest (prefill-heavy, higher M).

### 3.5 Marlin FP4 Scale Bug

sgl-kernel 0.3.20's `marlin_template.h` had FP4-specific `/2` in scale stride calculations, causing odd-indexed scale groups to produce wrong values (cos_sim=0.77). Fix: `patches/marlin_fp4_scale.patch` removes all FP4 special cases. After rebuild: cos_sim=0.999996.

### 3.6 Failed Alternatives

| Approach | Result | Why |
|----------|--------|-----|
| FP8 decode | 82 tok/s | 1.78x larger weights cancel bandwidth gain |
| flashinfer trtllm backend | segfault | cubin only for sm_100a, not sm_120 |
| Triton NVFP4 GEMV | 809 us/layer | 2.6x slower than CUTLASS (307 us) |
| Online NVFP4→FP8 dequant | 14.8 ms/step | 46% slower, dequant bandwidth overhead |
| Python-side scale adaptation | 17.4% error | group_size 16→32 geometric merge too lossy |

## 4. EAGLE-3 Speculative Decoding (Backlog)

### 4.1 Status: Not viable — blocked by quantization gap

Offline eval achieves ~60% first-token acceptance (eval_draft_ar.py), but SGLang integration shows ~0% accept rate. user_4813494d cause identified: **training/inference hidden state distribution mismatch**.

### 4.2 user_4813494d Cause Analysis

The draft model was trained on aux_hidden_states from the **BF16 target model**. In SGLang serving, the target runs as **NVFP4 quantized** (GPTQ + FP4). All 256 quantized Linear layers inject noise that propagates through layers 2, 10, 22 — the aux layers the draft model depends on. The draft model's fc projection and midlayer were never exposed to quantized hidden states during training, so predictions degrade to near-random.

Evidence:
- `eval_draft_ar.py` uses cached BF16 hidden states → 62.6% accept
- SGLang with NVFP4 target → accept_length consistently [0] across all verify steps
- Draft predictions differ from target predictions by unrelated token IDs (not close misses)

### 4.3 Implementation Summary

Files added/modified (all changes are behind EAGLE-only code paths, non-EAGLE inference unaffected):

| File | Change | Non-EAGLE impact |
|------|--------|-----------------|
| `minicpm.py` | aux_hidden capture at layers [3,11,23] (+1 offset); `set_eagle3_layers_to_capture` | None — `layers_to_capture=[]`, `capture_aux_hidden_states=False` by default |
| `minicpm_eagle3.py` | Draft model: fc(12288→4096) + midlayer + norm + lm_head(32000) | Only loaded in EAGLE mode |
| `minicpm_backend.py` | TARGET_VERIFY attention via FA3 paged attn | Branch not entered without EAGLE |
| `hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py` | SimpleGLA step-by-step verify with intermediate state caching; `has_initial_state` tensor→scalar fix | Verify path EAGLE-only; tensor fix benefits all modes |
| `eagle_info.py` | prepare_for_extend shift (standard EAGLE-3 convention) | EAGLE-only |
| `eagle_worker.py` | `mambaish_config` fix (was missing `minicpm_hybrid_config`); debug prints | EAGLE-only |

### 4.4 Key Technical Findings

1. **prepare_for_extend shift is intentional**: The left-shift-by-1 in `prepare_for_extend` makes the last position predict `t_{n+1}` (first speculative token) instead of `t_n` (= verified_id). Without shift, `d_1 = verified_id` always, giving 0% accept. This is standard EAGLE-3 behavior, not a bug.

2. **Training convention**: Step 0 uses unshifted embeds `(embed(t_q), fc(target_h_q))` → predict `t_{q+1}`. Step 1+ uses shifted `(embed(t_{q+1}), draft_h_q)` → predict `t_{q+2}`. The shift in `prepare_for_extend` approximately matches step 1.

3. **aux_hidden correctness verified**: `layers_to_capture = [val+1 for val in layer_ids]` captures layer output correctly because SGLang captures hidden BEFORE layer i executes. For `layer_ids=[2,10,22]`, captures at i=3,11,23 = output of layers 2,10,22. Matches HF `outputs.hidden_states[l+1]`.

4. **d2t mapping**: Stored as diffs (`d2t[i] = target_id - i`), recovered via `hot_token_id = d2t + arange(N)`. Verified correct.

### 4.5 Paths Forward (if revisited)

| Approach | Effort | Expected gain |
|----------|--------|--------------|
| Retrain draft on NVFP4 hidden states | High (re-collect phase 1 data with quantized model) | Should restore ~60% accept |
| Fine-tune draft with mixed BF16/NVFP4 hidden | Medium | Partial recovery |
| Run BF16 target + EAGLE draft | N/A (BF16 doesn't fit with draft model VRAM) | Would work but defeats purpose |
| Distill quantization-robust draft | High | Research-grade |

### 4.6 Recommendation

Park EAGLE-3 as a backlog item. Current Marlin hybrid decode already provides significant speedup (S1: 270s vs 650s baseline). EAGLE-3 would compound on top but requires retraining the draft model against the quantized target.

## 5. Medusa Speculative Decoding

### 5.1 Architecture

Medusa-1 approach: K ResBlock heads + frozen shared lm_head predict K draft tokens from target hidden states. All K+1 tokens (verified_id + K drafts) verified in a single TARGET_VERIFY forward pass. GLA intermediate states saved per-step for rollback on rejection.

- Training: `medusa/train.py` — freeze base model, train heads on cached hidden states
- Inference: `medusa_worker.py` — integrated into SGLang's speculative decoding framework
- Head structure: ResBlock (Linear + SiLU + residual) per head, ~33M params each

### 5.2 Key Findings

**K=1 is optimal for MiniCPM-SALA.** Head accuracy degrades rapidly:

| Head | Top1 Acc | Marginal value |
|------|----------|----------------|

## 5. Medusa Speculative Decoding

### 5.1 Architecture

Medusa-1 approach: K ResBlock heads + frozen shared lm_head predict K draft tokens from target hidden states. All K+1 tokens (verified_id + K drafts) verified in a single TARGET_VERIFY forward pass. GLA intermediate states saved per-step for rollback on rejection.

- Training: `medusa/train.py` — freeze base model, train heads on cached hidden states
- Inference: `medusa_worker.py` — integrated into SGLang's speculative decoding framework
- Head structure: ResBlock (Linear + SiLU + residual) per head, ~33M params each

### 5.2 Key Findings

**K=1 is optimal for MiniCPM-SALA.** Head accuracy degrades rapidly:

| Head | Top1 Acc | Marginal value |
|------|----------|----------------|
| head_0 | 35.6% | Primary contributor |
| head_1 | 21.0% | Only useful when head_0 hits (cascading) |
| head_2 | 14.4% | Negligible marginal gain |

K=3 gives tokens_per_step=1.543 vs K=1 ~1.356, but K=3 costs dtn=4 (4-token forward) vs K=1 dtn=2. GLA overhead scales linearly with dtn — the 2x reduction in overhead far outweighs the 0.19 extra tokens/step.

**Speed impact (local, K=1):**
- S1: 436s → 360s (17.4% faster) — significant, decode-dominated
- S8: improvement proportional
- Smax: slight regression — high concurrency means spec's extra KV slots and verify overhead dominate over single-request speedup. Future optimization target.

**Accuracy: zero degradation.** 131/150 eval comparison (spec vs non-spec): 79.59% vs 79.71%, within eval noise. Per-task: niah/fwe/cwe identical, mcq/qa differences symmetric (random).

### 5.3 Bugs Fixed

1. **K subset loading from checkpoint** — Checkpoint saved 3 heads but K=1 only needs 1. Original code overrode K to checkpoint value, causing tensor shape mismatch crash. Fix: load all checkpoint heads, copy only first K.

2. **Empty responses (two user_4813494d causes)**:
   - *Memory pressure*: Spec mode needs extra KV cache slots (dtn per request). With `mem-fraction-static=0.85`, requests returned empty. Fix: reduce to 0.80.
   - *CUDA graph verify buffer overflow*: `BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper.plan()` during CUDA graph capture used `kv_indices_buf[:total_pages]` where `total_pages` was based on `dense_len=8192` (~8196 pages). At replay, long sequences (e.g. 16K+) needed more pages → `ValueError: paged_kv_indices exceeds allocated buffer`. Fix: pass `kv_indices_buf[:bs * verify_max_pages]` at capture (full capacity based on `max_context_len=524288`). Zero runtime overhead — buffer already allocated at init (134 MB). See `minicpm_backend.py` line ~1808.

3. **Vectorized verify loop** — Original Python loop used per-request `.item()` calls (GPU→CPU sync). Replaced with batch tensor ops: 142x speedup on free_slots/seq_lens/last_tokens update. Correctness verified bit-exact across 24 test scenarios.

4. **Sparse k1/k2 allocation** — Normal decode allocates InfLLM-v2 sparse slots in `alloc_for_decode`, but Medusa skips that path. Added `_alloc_sparse_for_new_positions` to prevent over-free in `cache_finished_req`.

### 5.4 Sparse vs Full Attention Divergence (Non-issue)

TARGET_VERIFY uses FlashInfer full prefill (all KV, full head count), while normal decode uses InfLLM-v2 sparse attention (top-k KV subset, head group splitting tp_q_head_num//2). This produces slightly different logits — but empirically causes zero accuracy degradation, as the full-attention path is actually more "correct."

### 5.5 Training Configuration

Current best config (`medusa/train.py`):
- 1 head, 10 epochs, lr=1e-3, cosine schedule
- Train data: train + val splits merged (8539 files), eval on val_ood only (191 files)
- Best checkpoint selected by val_ood mean_accept_len
- Weights: ~32 MB (1 head) vs ~97 MB (3 heads)

### 5.6 Submission Integration

Medusa weights bundled in `demo-sala/data/medusa_best.pt`. `prepare_env.sh` sets:
```
--speculative-algorithm MEDUSA --speculative-num-steps 1
--speculative-draft-model-path ${SCRIPT_DIR}/data/medusa_best.pt
```
Package size: 225 MB (< 2 GB limit).

### 5.7 Future Work

- Retrain head on NVFP4 hidden states (current head trained on BF16 — works but suboptimal)
- Optimize high-concurrency (Smax) overhead: reduce per-request KV allocation cost, batch verify more efficiently
- Explore deeper head architecture (2-layer ResBlock) for higher head_0 accuracy

## 6. Sparse Decode Replay Investigation

The highest-potential remaining optimization appears to be MiniCPM sparse decode replay overhead rather than another matmul-only change.

### 6.1 Working Hypothesis

The main waste is in metadata orchestration around sparse FlashInfer decode:
- per-step replay-side metadata updates in `minicpm_backend.py`
- repeated wrapper `begin_forward()` refresh during CUDA graph replay
- full-buffer `fill_(-inf)` on `compress_k1` / `compress_k2`
- Python/CPU-side sparse metadata construction before kernels run

### 6.2 P0 Code Paths Under Review

- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_backend.py`
- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_sparse_utils.py`
- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/managers/schedule_batch.py`
- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_attention_kernels.py`

### 6.3 Immediate Questions

1. Can replay avoid `torch.cuda.synchronize()` after wrapper refresh?
2. Can sparse FlashInfer metadata be updated incrementally instead of rebuilt/copied each step?
3. Can compressed-K buffers stop doing full-range clears and switch to valid-range writes only?
4. Can decode metadata stay on-device and avoid repeated CPU tensor staging?
```

2. **CUDA graph verify buffer overflow** — `plan()`时用`dense_len`-based capacity (~8196 pages)，长序列replay时溢出

**找到了！**

`chunk_cache.py` 关键差异：

**demo-sala（有修复）**:
```python
# Skip freeing if indices are uninitialized (all zeros) - this happens during speculative decoding
# when sparse attention slots weren't allocated for draft tokens
k1_indices = k1_indices[k1_indices > 0]
if k1_indices.numel() > 0:
    self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.free(k1_indices)
```

**probe-sala（缺少修复）**:
```python
self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.free(k1_indices)  # 直接free，可能free零索引！
```

在speculative decoding时，draft tokens的sparse slots可能未分配（全零）。probe-sala会尝试free这些零索引，导致内存corruption → 空响应。

+# Medusa Empty Response Analysis
+
+Date: 2026-04-05
+
+## Scope
+
+This note records the current analysis of the empty-response issue observed when
+running speculative decoding via `eval/start_spec.sh` against the public eval
+set.
+
+The specific symptom is:
+
+- HTTP status is `200`
+- `finish_reason` is `"length"`
+- `usage.completion_tokens` is non-zero
+- `choices[0].message.content` is `null`
+
+This note focuses on the current speculative decoding path only. The non-spec
+baseline is intentionally not re-compared here.
+
+## Runtime Configuration
+
+The current repro path uses:
+
+- [eval/start_spec.sh](/user_4813494d/openbmb/eval/start_spec.sh)
+- `--speculative-algorithm MEDUSA`
+- `--speculative-num-steps 1`
+- draft weights at `/user_4813494d/openbmb/medusa/weights/best.pt`
+
+The active eval runner is:
+
+- [eval/run_public_eval_full.sh](/user_4813494d/openbmb/eval/run_public_eval_full.sh)
+- [eval/run_public_eval_full.py](/user_4813494d/openbmb/eval/run_public_eval_full.py)
+
+Current local eval script settings matter:
+
+- `max_tokens` in the eval script is currently `128`
+- therefore `finish_reason="length"` at `128` tokens is an observation window,
+  not evidence that the model naturally decided to stop there
+
+## Confirmed Facts
+
+### 1. The server itself returns `content: null`
+
+This is not a client parsing problem.
+
+Serial repro against failed samples produced raw responses such as:
+
+- [raw_0062.json](/user_4813494d/openbmb/outputs/probe_failed_20260405_180722/raw_0062.json)
+- [raw_0039.json](/user_4813494d/openbmb/outputs/probe_failed_20260405_180722/raw_0039.json)
+- [raw_0050.json](/user_4813494d/openbmb/outputs/probe_failed_20260405_180722/raw_0050.json)
+
+All show:
+
+- `message.content: null`
+- `finish_reason: "length"`
+- `completion_tokens: 128`
+
+The probe summary is in:
+
+- [summary.json](/user_4813494d/openbmb/outputs/probe_failed_20260405_180722/summary.json)
+
+### 2. `content: null` is caused by empty decoded text
+
+The OpenAI chat response builder sets `content=None` when `ret_item["text"]` is
+ empty:
+
+- [serving_chat.py](/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/entrypoints/openai/serving_chat.py#L809)
+
+The text is accumulated in `TokenizerManager` from `BatchStrOutput.output_strs`:
+
+- [tokenizer_manager.py](/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/managers/tokenizer_manager.py#L1523)
+
+Therefore `content: null` means the decoded string reaching OpenAI serving is
+empty.
+
+### 3. The failing requests are not producing "no tokens"; they are producing token id `0`
+
+For a failing sample, repeating the same request with `skip_special_tokens=false`
+changes the response from `content=null` to a long `"<unk>"` string.
+
+Using the lower-level `/generate` endpoint on the same failing sample returned:
+
+- `output_ids = [0, 0, 0, ...]`
+- length equals `completion_tokens`
+- decoded text is repeated `"<unk>"`
+
+For this tokenizer:
+
+- `unk_token = "<unk>"`
+- `unk_token_id = 0`
+- decoding token `0` with `skip_special_tokens=True` yields `""`
+
+So the empty-response symptom is actually:
+
+- speculative decoding generates repeated token id `0`
+- the tokenizer decodes it to an empty string under `skip_special_tokens=True`
+- OpenAI serving turns the empty string into `content=None`
+
+### 4. The issue is deterministic for specific requests
+
+Single-request serial repro still fails for known bad indices such as:
+
+- `62`
+- `39`
+- `50`
+
+This rules out "only happens under high concurrency" as the main explanation.
+
+Adjacent requests can still behave normally under the same server process, so
+the failure is request-conditional rather than a total server failure.
+
+### 5. Captured Medusa debug state shows NaNs, not merely bad token choices
+
+The local debug instrumentation was extended to capture cases where
+`next_token_id == 0`. Reproducing failed samples then produced capture files in:
+
+- [/tmp/empty_response_debug/capture_1_183839_req0.pt](/tmp/empty_response_debug/capture_1_183839_req0.pt)
+- [/tmp/empty_response_debug/capture_2_183839_req0.pt](/tmp/empty_response_debug/capture_2_183839_req0.pt)
+- [/tmp/empty_response_debug/capture_3_183839_req0.pt](/tmp/empty_response_debug/capture_3_183839_req0.pt)
+
+Observed fields from these captures:
+
+- `verified_id = 0`
+- `draft_tokens = [0]`
+- `target_preds_all_positions = [0, 0]`
+- `hs_norm_this_req = nan`
+- `output_hs_has_nan = [True, True]`
+- `logits_stats[*].has_nan = True`
+
+This means the failure path is:
+
+- hidden states entering Medusa decode are already `NaN`
+- verify logits are `NaN`
+- `argmax` degenerates to token id `0`
+- decoded text becomes repeated `"<unk>"`
+
+This is a much stronger statement than "speculative decoding picked a bad token".
+
+### 6. The first bad token exists before the first Medusa verify correction
+
+In the earliest captures:
+
+- `req_output_len = 1`
+- `verified_id = 0`
+- `seq_len_before` equals the request prefill length
+
+This strongly suggests that the first output token produced after prefill is
+already `0`, before the first speculative decode round has a chance to recover.
+
+Operationally, that points suspicion upstream from Medusa verify itself and
+toward the prefill/extend path that Medusa uses to obtain:
+
+- the first `verified_id`
+- the `LAST` hidden state used for the draft head
+
+That path is:
+
+- [medusa_worker.py](/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/medusa_worker.py#L354)
+
+where Medusa explicitly forces:
+
+- `model_worker_batch.capture_hidden_mode = CaptureHiddenMode.LAST`
+
+### 7. The issue is strongly tied to chunked prefill
+
+This was verified by changing only the prefill chunking configuration while
+keeping `MEDUSA` enabled.
+
+Observed behavior:
+
+- With the usual config:
+  - `chunked_prefill_size=8192`
+  - failed sample `idx=62` returns first token `0`
+  - `_forward_extend` capture shows `NaN` hidden state and `NaN` logits
+
+- With enlarged prefill config:
+  - `chunked_prefill_size=32768`
+  - `max_prefill_tokens=32768`
+  - the same `idx=62` request returns a normal first token (`"<think>"`)
+
+This is strong evidence that the bug is not a generic "all sparse prefill is
+broken" issue. It is much more likely to be an interaction between:
+
+- sparse prefill
+- Medusa's `_forward_extend`
+- chunked prefill state / metadata carry-over
+
```
