```
- chunked sparse prefill in MiniCPM
- a query/block boundary transition inside the final prefill chunk
- and Medusa consuming the resulting corrupted first token + last hidden state

This points more to speculative hidden-state capture / sparse chunked prefill
state handling than to an OpenAI API bug.

## Later Findings

Further instrumentation narrowed the first real corruption site much more
aggressively than the earlier sparse-metadata hypothesis.

### 10. The first true NaN is produced inside an MLP, not in Medusa verify

With finer probes enabled on `MiniCPMDecoderLayer.forward()` and
`MiniCPMMLP.forward()`, a stable bad repro (`idx=90`, spec server,
`max_tokens=1`, `skip_special_tokens=false`) produced:

- first hidden-state NaN at `post_mlp`
- finite MLP input row
- finite `gate_up_proj` output row
- finite SiLU-and-mul activation row
- fully-NaN `down_proj` output row

Relevant captures:

- [/tmp/minicpm_sparse_probe/first_nan_hidden_stage.pt](/tmp/minicpm_sparse_probe/first_nan_hidden_stage.pt)
- [/tmp/minicpm_sparse_probe/first_nan_mlp.pt](/tmp/minicpm_sparse_probe/first_nan_mlp.pt)
- [/tmp/minicpm_sparse_probe/first_nan_qkv_layer.pt](/tmp/minicpm_sparse_probe/first_nan_qkv_layer.pt)

The strongest captured evidence was:

- `input_inf_rows = []`
- `gate_up_inf_rows = []`
- `act_inf_rows = []`
- `output_nan_rows = [3030, 3077]`
- `input_row_absmax = 5.3125`
- `gate_up_row_absmax = 6.0`
- `act_row_absmax = 7.53125`
- `output_row_sample = [nan, nan, ...]`

This means the failure is not explained by normal activation blow-up before the
MLP output projection. The first hard failure observed in this repro is the
MLP `down_proj` output itself.

### 11. The bad operation matches the NVFP4 CUTLASS GEMM path

The failing MLP output projection runs through `RowParallelLinear`, which calls
`ModelOptFp4LinearMethod.apply()`.

For the failing extend/prefill call, the effective row count was much larger
than the hybrid Marlin threshold:

- extend chunk shape had `x_m = 3455`
- the old startup script used `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=48`

Therefore the failing path was not the Marlin branch. It was the default
NVFP4 CUTLASS path in:

- [modelopt_quant.py](/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/modelopt_quant.py)

Operationally, the new working hypothesis became:

- long-context chunked prefill/extend under spec reaches a bad case in
  `cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm`
- the GEMM output row becomes all-NaN
- later layers propagate NaNs
- Medusa finally exposes that corruption as repeated token id `0`

### 12. Switching spec to full NVFP4 Marlin eliminates the failures

This was validated with a materially different execution path, not a small
parameter tweak.

Spec server was restarted with:

- `SGLANG_FORCE_NVFP4_MARLIN=1`
- `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=0`

That forces NVFP4 linears to use the Marlin fallback instead of the CUTLASS
FP4 GEMM path.

Observed results:

- previously stable-bad first-token repros now all recovered to `"<think>"`
  for `idx=62/72/85/90/100`
- a concurrent run over historical bad indices
  `39/50/62/72/73/84/85/90/91/92/100/112` with `max_tokens=128` produced
  `bad_count=0`
- `eagle/debug_concurrent_unk.py --concurrency 8 --num-requests 8` also
  produced `OK=8 EMPTY=0 UNK=0 ERR=0`

This is the strongest end-to-end evidence collected so far. It shows:

- the spec empty-response problem is not fundamentally caused by Medusa logic
- it is strongly tied to the NVFP4 CUTLASS GEMM implementation used on the spec
  path
- forcing Marlin removes both the stable bad cases and the concurrent
  `<unk>/null` failures seen during investigation

## Implemented Mitigation

The spec startup scripts were updated to force Marlin on the speculative path:

- [start_spec.sh](/user_4813494d/openbmb/eval/start_spec.sh)
- [start_spec_debug.sh](/user_4813494d/openbmb/eval/start_spec_debug.sh)

They now export:

- `SGLANG_FORCE_NVFP4_MARLIN=1`
- `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=0`

This is intentionally scoped to the spec launch scripts, because the observed
correctness regression was investigated and verified on the speculative serving
path triggered by those scripts.

## Performance Constraint

This mitigation is a correctness stopgap, not the final competition-ready
solution.

Why:

- forcing `SGLANG_FORCE_NVFP4_MARLIN=1` does not only affect decode
- it also changes the NVFP4 linear path used during prefill/extend
- therefore long-context speculative prefill no longer uses the default
  CUTLASS FP4 GEMM path

For competition serving, that tradeoff is important:

- the current mitigation removes the `content=null` / repeated-`<unk>` failure
- but it is expected to sacrifice part of the CUTLASS prefill performance gain

So the current state should be understood as:

- `full Marlin on spec` is a validated workaround
- it is useful for proving the user_4813494d-cause direction and restoring correctness
- it is not the desired final optimization strategy

The next-stage target should instead be a narrower fix that preserves CUTLASS
prefill performance, for example:

- repair the underlying CUTLASS FP4 correctness bug
- or add a much smaller fallback only on the specific speculative bad path
- or limit fallback to the minimal affected operator / phase instead of all
  NVFP4 linears

## Current Conclusion

The current best-supported user_4813494d cause is:

- spec serving exposed a correctness bug in the NVFP4 CUTLASS GEMM path during
  long-context chunked prefill/extend
- that bug first becomes visible as all-NaN MLP `down_proj` output rows
- downstream layers and Medusa decode then collapse to repeated token id `0`
  and finally `content=null`

The currently validated fix is:

- force NVFP4 Marlin fallback for the spec startup path

Based on the targeted serial repros and the concurrent regression runs above,
this removes the empty-response issue that motivated this investigation.

However, from a competition perspective, this should be treated as:

- a user_4813494d-cause-confirming workaround
- not yet the final high-performance answer

The remaining engineering goal is:

- keep CUTLASS prefill where it is safe and fast
- while eliminating the speculative empty-response bug completely

## Cross-Validation On Other Failed Indices

Additional serial validation was run against the failed indices from the public
eval output, using the same `MEDUSA` server and forcing:

- `max_tokens=1`
- `skip_special_tokens=false`

This split the failed set into two different observed behaviors.

### A. Stable prefill-failure subgroup

The following failed indices immediately produced `"<unk>"` as the very first
token in serial repro:

- `62`
- `72`
- `85`
- `90`
- `100`

For these requests, the evidence was consistent:

- first token was already `0`
- `medusa_worker._forward_extend` captured `extend_hidden_nan`
- `next_token_logits` already contained `NaN`
- the final prefill chunk in `/tmp/medusa_collect` contained `NaN`
- `/tmp/minicpm_sparse_debug` showed the first `NaN` query row inside the last
  prefill chunk, after the local sparse window

Observed first-NaN rows from the captured last chunk included:

- `2368` for `idx=62`
- `2944` for `idx=72`
- `2944` for `idx=85`
- `3008` for `idx=90`
- `3020` for `idx=100`

Representative sparse metadata transitions at those rows included:

- `6144 -> 6081`
- `6112 -> 6113`
- `6118 -> 6119`

The exact boundary is not fixed to one single row such as `2304`, but it does
stay in the same suspicious regime:

- after `window_size=2048`
- aligned with later `block_size=64` block boundaries
- within the final chunk of chunked sparse prefill

This makes the current sparse-prefill metadata hypothesis stronger, not weaker:
the bug appears to be a family of boundary failures in the same sparse region,
not a single hard-coded row.

### B. Non-stable / not-currently-prefill-failing subgroup

The following failed indices did **not** immediately fail under at least one
serial `max_tokens=1` repro:

- `39`
- `50`
- `73`
- `84`
- `91`
- `92`
- `112`

For these requests, at least one serial repro produced:

- first token `"<think>"`
- no `extend_hidden_nan`
- no `NaN` in `/tmp/medusa_collect`
- no sparse debug dump

This means the full failed set is not explained by one single deterministic
"prefill always breaks" mechanism.

Instead, the current evidence supports a narrower statement:

- one subgroup is a deterministic or near-deterministic sparse chunked-prefill
  corruption issue
- another subgroup is unstable, timing-sensitive, or otherwise not currently
  reproduced by a single `max_tokens=1` serial run

## Follow-Up On The Non-Stable Subgroup

To probe whether the second subgroup only fails later in decode, representative
cases were re-run with:

- `max_tokens=128`
- `skip_special_tokens=false`

Observed outcomes:

- `idx=39` produced normal text for all 128 tokens in one serial repro and
  generated no empty-response capture at all
- `idx=91`, which had previously produced a normal first token in a
  `max_tokens=1` serial repro, later flipped on a fresh run and immediately
  produced `extend_hidden_nan`, `verified_id=0`, and repeated `"<unk>"`
  captures from output length `1`

So the currently safest interpretation is:

- some failed indices are stable prefill-corruption cases
- some failed indices sit near an instability boundary and do not reproduce the
  same way every time

That weakens the earlier blanket claim that "all failed indices are the same
user_4813494d cause", but it does not weaken the main user_4813494d-cause candidate for the
stable subgroup.

## Current Best Narrow Statement

The strongest verified statement as of now is:

- there is a real bug in MiniCPM sparse chunked prefill under the `MEDUSA`
  server path
- this bug can corrupt the last-chunk hidden states before speculative verify
  begins
- once that happens, Medusa starts from `verified_id=0`, verify logits become
  `NaN`, and the output degenerates into repeated token `0`

What is **not** yet fully proven is whether every original `prediction=None`
sample belongs to that exact same mechanism. Current evidence says no: at least
part of the failed set is unstable or requires additional triggering
conditions.

## New Narrowing: TopK Is Not The First Corruption Site

Further probes were added to compare bad and good samples at the same sparse
rows.

For bad samples such as `idx=90`, the first anomalous row showed:

- `topk_idx[head0] == [1, 2, 3, ..., 96]`
- identical degeneration across both head groups

For nearby successful samples such as `idx=92`, the same rows showed:

- normal sparse block selections
- no contiguous `[1..96]` degeneration

This ruled out the earlier too-coarse theory that the sparse page-table padding
itself was the first bad event.

The crucial next probe captured `compressed_attention()` internals for a bad
sample and showed:

- `first_contiguous_row = 2944` or `3008` depending on sample
- `score_has_nan = True`
- `block_score_has_nan = True`

with row slices showing:

- the row just before the boundary still had finite `block_score`
- at the first bad row, `block_score` became almost entirely `NaN`
- `topk()` then degenerated into the default sorted prefix `[1..96]`

So the causal order is now:

- compressed-attention stage output becomes `NaN`
- `block_score.topk()` degenerates to `[1..96]`
- sparse page table is built from those bad indices
- attention output becomes `NaN`
- Medusa extend starts from `verified_id=0`

This means `get_block_table_v2` is **not** the earliest corruption site.
It only consumes already-corrupted Top-K inputs.

## New Narrowing: `q/k/k2` Inputs Are Already `NaN`

The same `compressed_attention()` probe was extended to capture input finiteness
for bad samples.

For a reproduced bad `idx=90` request:

- `q_has_nan = True`
- `k_has_nan = True`
- `k2_has_nan = True`
- `score_has_nan = True`
- `block_score_has_nan = True`

Therefore the compressed-attention code path is not producing `NaN` from clean
inputs. It is receiving already-corrupted Q/K inputs.

This pushes the first real corruption site further upstream:

- before sparse Top-K scoring
- before sparse page-table construction
- before Medusa verify

## New Narrowing: First QKV `NaN` Appears At Layer 9

A layer probe was added in `MiniCPMBackend.forward_extend` to record the first
attention layer whose Q/K/V tensors already contain `NaN`.

For bad `idx=90`, the first hit was:

- `layer_id = 9`

The model config shows the early layer pattern is:

- layer `0`: `minicpm4`
- layers `1..8`: `lightning-attn`
- layer `9`: `minicpm4`

So the first standard sparse-attention layer (`layer 9`) is the first place
where Q/K/V are observed as `NaN`. This strongly suggests the actual corruption
happens **before or during the preceding lightning-attn / GLA stack**, and only
becomes visible when the model re-enters a `minicpm4` sparse-attention layer.

That re-centers suspicion toward:

- hybrid lightning-attention state handling across chunked prefill boundaries
- GLA recurrent state carry-over between chunks
- the handoff from the last lightning-attn layer into the next minicpm4 layer

and away from:

- OpenAI serving
- Medusa verify logic itself
- sparse page-table construction as the primary user_4813494d cause

## Non-Spec Versus Medusa
```

```
## Non-Spec Versus Medusa

A direct comparison was run on April 5, 2026 using:

- the same model
- the same `minicpm_flashinfer` backend
- the same `chunked_prefill_size=8192`
- the same `--dense-as-sparse`
- the same quantization
- and with `MEDUSA` removed, while keeping the rest of the serving config aligned

### Stable Medusa-failing samples under non-spec

For the subgroup that often fails under `MEDUSA` prefill:

- `62`
- `72`
- `85`
- `90`
- `100`

non-spec serial `max_tokens=1` results were much healthier overall:

- `62`: `"<think>"`
- `85`: `"<think>"`
- `90`: `"<think>"`
- `100`: `"<think>"`
- `72`: one run returned `"<unk>"`, but later runs returned `"<think>"`

Additional repeated non-spec trials showed:

- `idx=90`: `5/5` runs returned `"<think>"`
- `idx=72`: not stable; repeated runs produced both `"<unk>"` and `"<think>"`

So the current evidence is:

- the issue is **not strictly Medusa-only**
- but `MEDUSA` makes the failure substantially easier to reproduce and much more
  stable on several indices

### Non-spec long response check

For `idx=72`, non-spec was also re-run with:

- `max_tokens=128`
- default `skip_special_tokens=True`

One serial run produced a normal textual answer and stopped normally, rather
than returning `content=null`.

### Current practical interpretation

The best current reading is:

- there is an underlying long-context numerical instability in the model/backend
  path
- `MEDUSA` exposes and amplifies it because speculative prefill/extend depends
  on the exact first verified token and the captured last hidden state
- non-spec can still touch the same instability boundary, but so far it appears
  much less frequent and much less deterministic

## Most Suspicious Code Paths

### 1. Medusa verify path

Medusa builds a flattened verify batch:

- [medusa_worker.py](/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/medusa_worker.py#L417)

It then runs target verify and directly takes:

- `target_preds = all_logits.argmax(dim=-1)`

at:

- [medusa_worker.py](/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/medusa_worker.py#L460)

If verify logits are garbage or near-constant, `argmax` will deterministically
pick index `0`, which matches the observed output ids.

However, the new capture evidence suggests the corruption may begin even earlier,
before this function starts consuming `spec_info.hidden_states`.

### 2. Medusa verify input / batch preparation

Medusa uses a dedicated `MedusaVerifyInput`:

- [eagle_info.py](/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_info.py#L828)

and fills `batch.input_ids` plus KV mappings in:

- [eagle_info.py](/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_info.py#L864)

Any mismatch in:

- verify positions
- KV slot mapping
- seq lens interpretation
- batch-local state reuse

could poison the target verify logits.
```

> AGENT

找到了！`cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm` 就在 `common_ops.abi3.so` 里，还有 `cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm_sm100a_sm120a`。

所以替换 .so 同时修复了两个问题：
1. Marlin FP4 scale bug
2. CUTLASS FP4 GEMM 的 NaN bug（新版源码）

这就是为什么替换 .so 能消除空响应。

现在问题是：probe-sala 提交时 .so 替换确认成功了（md5 一致），但还是有 6 个空响应。本地没问题，平台有问题——本地和平台用的是不同的量化模型吧？本地用的是已有的 128-sample 量化模型，平台用的是 probe-sala 1-sample 量化出来的模型？

对！替换 .so 文件成功 ≠ sglang server 加载了新 .so。

我们的 probe 只验证了 prepare_model.sh 里的 Python 进程加载的是新.so，但 **sglang server 是平台单独启动的另一个进程**，它加载的是什么完全没有验证过。

要在 sglang server 启动时验证加载的是哪个 .so，需要在 sglang 代码里加日志。你想怎么验证？

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/probe-sala/prepare_model.sh",
  "old_string": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -eo pipefail\n# probe prepare_model.sh — quantize, self-eval with Medusa, email results, exit 1\n# NOTE: removed -u to avoid silent exit on unbound $2\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n\necho \"[probe] start $(date '+%F %T')\"\necho \"[probe] args: $*\"\n\n# Parse --input / --output (safe version)\nINPUT=\"\"\nOUTPUT=\"\"\nwhile [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do\n    case \"$1\" in\n        --input)  INPUT=\"${2:-}\";  shift; shift || true ;;\n        --output) OUTPUT=\"${2:-}\"; shift; shift || true ;;\n        *) shift ;;\n    esac\ndone\n\necho \"[probe] input=$INPUT output=$OUTPUT\"\n\nif [ -z \"$INPUT\" ] || [ -z \"$OUTPUT\" ]; then\n    echo \"[probe] ERROR: --input and --output required\" >&2\n    exit 1\nfi\n\n# ── Quantize (identical to demo-sala) ──\nexport PYTORCH_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=\":4096:8\"\n\n# ── Pre-quant email ──\necho \"[probe] sending pre-quant email...\"\npython3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_email.py\" 2>&1 || { echo \"[probe] pre-quant email FAILED, exit\"; exit 1; }\n\necho \"[probe] starting quantization (GPTQ+FourOverSix, 48K, loguniform128)\"\npython3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/preprocess_model.py\" --input \"$INPUT\" --output \"$OUTPUT\" 2>&1 | tee \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/quant_log.txt\"\necho \"[probe] quantization done $(date '+%F %T')\"\n\n# ── Self-eval + email ──\necho \"[probe] starting probe_eval...\"\npython3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_eval.py\" \"$OUTPUT\" 2>&1 || true\n\necho \"[probe] probe complete $(date '+%F %T'), exiting 1 to skip official eval\"\nexit 1",
  "new_string": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -eo pipefail\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n\necho \"[probe] start $(date '+%F %T')\"\n\n# 触发 sglang import，打印 common_ops.so md5，然后发邮件 exit\npython3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_email.py\" 2>&1 || { echo \"[probe] email FAILED\"; exit 1; }\n\necho \"[probe] done, exit 1\"\nexit 1"
}

等等，probe_email.py 里 import sgl_kernel 打印的是 prepare_model.sh 的进程，不是 sglang server 进程。用户要的是在 prepare_model.sh 里**启动 sglang**，让 server 进程本身 import sglang 打印 md5：

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/probe-sala/prepare_model.sh",
  "old_string": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -eo pipefail\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n\necho \"[probe] start $(date '+%F %T')\"\n\n# 触发 sglang import，打印 common_ops.so md5，然后发邮件 exit\npython3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_email.py\" 2>&1 || { echo \"[probe] email FAILED\"; exit 1; }\n\necho \"[probe] done, exit 1\"\nexit 1",
  "new_string": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -eo pipefail\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n\necho \"[probe] start $(date '+%F %T')\"\n\n# 空启动 sglang server，触发 import sglang，打印 common_ops.so md5，然后失败退出\n# --model-path 不存在没关系，import 阶段就会打印 md5\npython3 -m sglang.launch_server --model-path /nonexistent --port 30000 2>&1 | head -30 || true\n\npython3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_email.py\" 2>&1 || { echo \"[probe] email FAILED\"; exit 1; }\n\necho \"[probe] done, exit 1\"\nexit 1"
}
```

好。从日志看：

`!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! debug !!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!` 和 `[sgl_kernel] common_ops loaded md5=4f9ce8823ad4daa8aedbcc5141b33cf8` 是 `sglang.launch_server` 进程打印的，md5 正确。

**server 进程加载的 .so 是我们的修复版，确认了。**

然后 server 因为 `/nonexistent` 挂了，eval 没跑。

现在 .so 问题排除了，回到原点：根据文档，那 6 个空响应中有一部分本来就是**不稳定的**——"some failed indices sit near an instability boundary and do not reproduce the same way every time"。

可能不是固定 bug，是数值不稳定性的偶发现象。重新提交完整的 probe-sala eval，结果可能不一样。

> AGENT

默认值是 **0**（全 CUTLASS，没有 Marlin）。

如果平台 server 进程没有继承 `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=36`，就全走 CUTLASS。

但你说替换 .so 就能修复——说明我们的 .so 里的 CUTLASS 实现修复了 NaN。让我确认 `cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm` 到底是从 `common_ops.abi3.so` 注册的还是 `flash_ops.abi3.so`：

  "new_string": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -eo pipefail\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n\necho \"[probe] start $(date '+%F %T')\"\necho \"[probe] args: $*\"\n\nINPUT=\"\"\nOUTPUT=\"\"\nwhile [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do\n    case \"$1\" in\n        --input)  INPUT=\"${2:-}\";  shift; shift || true ;;\n        --output) OUTPUT=\"${2:-}\"; shift; shift || true ;;\n        *) shift ;;\n    esac\ndone\n\necho \"[probe] input=$INPUT output=$OUTPUT\"\n\nif [ -z \"$INPUT\" ] || [ -z \"$OUTPUT\" ]; then\n    echo \"[probe] ERROR: --input and --output required\" >&2\n    exit 1\nfi\n\nexport PYTORCH_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=\":4096:8\"\n\necho \"[probe] sending pre-quant email...\"\npython3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_email.py\" 2>&1 || { echo \"[probe] pre-quant email FAILED, exit\"; exit 1; }\n\necho \"[probe] starting quantization...\"\npython3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/preprocess_model.py\" --input \"$INPUT\" --output \"$OUTPUT\" 2>&1 | tee \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/quant_log.txt\"\necho \"[probe] quantization done $(date '+%F %T')\"\n\necho \"[probe] starting probe_eval...\"\npython3 \"${SCRIPT_DIR}/probe_eval.py\" \"$OUTPUT\" 2>&1 || true\n\necho \"[probe] probe complete $(date '+%F %T'), exiting 1\"\nexit 1"
}

NVFP4 CUTLASS FP4 GEMM（`cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm`）在**长上下文 chunked prefill** 时输出 NaN\n2. NaN 传播到 Medusa hidden states → logits 全 NaN → argmax 退化为 token id 0\n3. token 0 = `<unk>`，`skip_special_tokens=True` → decoded text 为空 → `content: null`\n\n`cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm` 的 C++ 实现位于 `sgl_kernel/sm100/common_ops.abi3.so`。\n\n---\n\n## .so 情况\n\n| 版本 | md5 | 大小 |\n|------|-----|------|\n| 平台原始（有 NaN bug） | `d62293b65115e33e2687299a09fd0fa9` | 261 MB |\n| 我们的修复版（本地编译） | `4f9ce8823ad4daa8aedbcc5141b33cf8` | 78 MB |\n\n大小差异原因：我们编译时只保留了 sm_120a，平台原始包含多个架构。功能上 sm_120a 已足够。\n\nprepare_env.sh 会替换 .so，已确认：\n- shell 层面替换成功\n- `prepare_model.sh` 里的 Python 进程加载了修复版（md5 一致）\n\n---\n\n## 未确认的关键环节\n\n**sglang server 进程加载的是哪个 .so？**\n\n目前所有 md5 验证都是在 `prepare_model.sh` 的 Python 子进程里做的，不是 server 进程本身。  \nserver 的输出写到 `server_log.txt`，从未被收集到邮件中。\n\n最新改动（尚未提交结果）：\n- `sglang/__init__.py` 加了启动时打印 common_ops md5\n- `probe_eval.py` 邮件中加入 `server_log.txt` 前 200 行\n\n---\n\n## 当前代码状态\n\n### probe-sala/prepare_env.sh\n- 安装自定义 sglang（editable install）\n- 替换 `sm100/common_ops.abi3.so`\n- export `SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS`（含 Medusa 参数）\n- export `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=36`\n\n### probe-sala/probe_eval.py `start_server()`\n- 明确传 `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD=36` 给 server 进程环境变量\n\n### `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD` 读取位置\n```\ndemo-sala/sglang/.../modelopt_quant.py:158\n_MARLIN_HYBRID_THRESHOLD = int(os.environ.get(\"SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD\", \"0\"))\n```\n默认值为 0（全 CUTLASS）。如果 server 进程没有继承该变量，Marlin 不生效，但 CUTLASS 修复了则仍无 NaN。\n\n---\n\n## 下一步\n\n1. **提交最新 probe-sala**，收到邮件后看 `=== SERVER LOG (first 200 lines) ===`：\n   - 是否有 `[sgl_kernel] common_ops loaded md5=4f9ce8823ad4daa8aedbcc5141b33cf8`\n   - 如果没有 → server 加载的是原始 .so，这是空响应的原因\n   - 如果有 → .so 没问题，空响应原因另找\n\n2. 如果 server 加载的是原始 .so：\n   - 原因可能是 editable install 的 sglang 路径问题，或 server 进程不继承 prepare_env.sh 的状态\n   - 解法：在 SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS 或 server 启动命令里加 `LD_PRELOAD` 或其他方式强制加载正确 .so\n\n3. 如果 server 加载的是修复版 .so 但仍有空响应：\n   - 检查 server_log.txt 里是否有其他报错\n   - 考虑回退到 `SGLANG_FORCE_NVFP4_MARLIN=1`（全 Marlin workaround，损失 prefill 性能）\n\n---\n\n## 相关文件\n\n| 文件 | 说明 |\n|------|------|\n| `docs/medusa-empty-response-analysis-20260405.md` | 完整空响应调查记录（git history，已删除） |\n| `probe-sala/prepare_env.sh` | 平台环境初始化，含 .so 替换 |\n| `probe-sala/probe_eval.py` | 自评估脚本，启动 server + 跑 eval + 发邮件 |\n| `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/__init__.py` | 加了 common_ops md5 打印 |\n| `probe-sala/sglang/python/sglang/__init__.py` | 同上 |\n| `/user_4813494d/probe-sala.tar.gz` | 最新打包（89MB），待提交 |\n"
}
